//! Creation and transition risk questions, independent of authored invariants.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — these gates demand review, never infer a specification.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr, Stmt, WriteOp, ProgramKind, TypeKind};
use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
fn note(out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,repair:&str){out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,repair).guide("security"));}
fn walk(e:&Expr,mut f:impl FnMut(&Expr)){let mut e=e.clone();super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|f(e));}
fn uses(e:&Expr,arg:&str)->bool{let mut found=false;walk(e,|e|if matches!(e,Expr::Name{name,..} if name==arg){found=true;});found}
fn conjunction(e:&Expr)->Vec<&Expr>{match e{Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="and"=>conjunction(left).into_iter().chain(conjunction(right)).collect(),_=>vec![e]}}
fn conditions(p:&ast::ProgramDecl)->Vec<&Expr>{p.body.iter().filter_map(|s|if let Stmt::Require{condition,..}=s{Some(condition)}else{None}).collect()}
fn reads(e:&Expr)->BTreeSet<String>{let mut out=BTreeSet::new();walk(e,|e|match e{Expr::Read(r)|Expr::Exists{read:r,..}=>{out.insert(r.table.clone());},_=>{}});out}
fn nonnull(p:&ast::ProgramDecl,arg:&str)->bool{p.body.iter().filter_map(|s|if let Stmt::Require{condition,code,..}=s&&code!="$advice"{Some(condition)}else{None}).flat_map(conjunction).any(|e|match e{
    Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..} if op=="!="=>[(left.as_ref(),right.as_ref()),(right.as_ref(),left.as_ref())].iter().any(|(a,b)|matches!(a,Expr::Name{name,..} if name==arg)&&matches!(b,Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..})),_=>false,
})}
fn field_value<'a>(f:&'a ast::ObjectField)->Option<&'a Expr>{f.value.as_ref()}
struct Write<'a>{p:&'a ast::ProgramDecl,op:WriteOp,table:String,fields:Vec<ast::ObjectField>,site:Site,context:ast::ProgramDecl,gate:RuleExpr}
fn bound_role(catalog:&Catalog,info:&super::super::catalog::TableInfo,expr:&Expr,recipient:&str)->Option<RuleExpr>{
    let Expr::Exists{read,..}=expr else{return None;};
    // Bind a positive recipient probe after substituting only its principal.
    if !read.keys.iter().any(|k|matches!(&k.value,Expr::Name{name,..} if name==recipient)){return None;}
    let mut value=expr.clone();super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |e|if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&name==recipient{*name="viewer".into();});
    super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),site:expr.site().clone(),value}).ok()
}
pub(super) fn notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let docs=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|crate::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=Catalog::build(docs.clone(),&mut Vec::new());let mut writes=Vec::new();
    for p in docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
        for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
            let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=write else{continue;};
            let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&table)else{continue;};
            let mut requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context);
            for condition in conditions(&context){let mut value=condition.clone();
                super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |e|if let Expr::Member{base,..}=e&&reads(base).contains(&table){*base=Box::new(Expr::Focal{site:site.clone()});});
                if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(&catalog,info,&ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value,site:site.clone()}){requirements.push(rule);}
            }
            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];let rule=if op==WriteOp::Update{t.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else{t.rules.insert.clone()};
            writes.push(Write{p,op,table,fields,site,context,gate:RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([rule]).collect())});
        }
    }
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
    for w in &writes{
        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&w.table];let creating=matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert);
        if creating{
            // Only unconditional requirements before the FIRST creating write
            // establish mandatory parent evidence; a conditional child's guard cannot.
            let first=writes.iter().find(|a|std::ptr::eq(a.p,w.p)&&matches!(a.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert));
            for f in &w.fields{let Some(value)=field_value(f)else{continue;};let Some(kind)=t.columns.get(&f.name)else{continue;};
                for arg in &w.p.args{if !uses(value,&arg.name){continue;}
                    if arg.type_.optional&&matches!(arg.type_.kind,TypeKind::Image{..}|TypeKind::File{..})&&kind.contains_asset()&&!first.is_some_and(|a|nonnull(&a.context,&arg.name))&&seen.insert((w.p.name.clone(),format!("upload:{}",arg.name))){
                        note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE",format!("{}: optional {} can omit media while creating {}; including conditional child evidence, does the spec require this upload?",w.p.name,arg.name,w.table),"make the creating mutation's upload argument required, or require it before creating the parent; intentional optionality needs an individual security disposition");
                    }
                    if f.name.to_lowercase().contains("reason")&&matches!(arg.type_.kind,TypeKind::Text{min:None|Some(0),..})&&seen.insert((w.p.name.clone(),format!("reason:{}",f.name))){
                        note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_CREATION_REASON",format!("{}: {}.{} accepts an empty/whitespace reason at creation",w.p.name,w.table,f.name),"if the workflow requires justification, use text 1..N (non-whitespace minimum), including on optional reasons when supplied");
                    }
                    let money=matches!(kind.required(),ColumnType::Money{..})||["price","charge","fee","amount"].iter().any(|word|f.name.to_lowercase().contains(word))&&matches!(kind.required(),ColumnType::Int|ColumnType::IntRange{..}|ColumnType::Decimal{..});
                    if money&&arg.type_.optional&&kind.is_optional()&&seen.insert((w.p.name.clone(),format!("money:{}",f.name))){note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_CREATION_MONEY",format!("{}: {}.{} permits omitted money at creation; is the required amount recorded?",w.p.name,w.table,f.name),"model the amount and require it at the workflow boundary when the spec requires payment or a price");}
                }
                if matches!(value,Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Bool(true),..})&&["paid","approv","payment"].iter().any(|s|f.name.to_lowercase().contains(s)){
                    note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_CONSTANT_APPROVAL",format!("{}: {}.{} is stored as constant true; what verifies the payment/approval and its amount? (name-based question)",w.p.name,w.table,f.name),"record the required money/evidence and independently authorized decision; confirm constant flags against the spec");
                }
            }
        }
        // A global role plus viewer attribution does not model ownership of this resource.
        if w.op==WriteOp::Update&&w.fields.iter().any(|f|matches!(field_value(f),Some(Expr::Name{name,..}) if name=="viewer")&&matches!(t.columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Principal))){
            let safe=RuleExpr::And(conditions(&w.context).into_iter().filter_map(|e|super::super::schema::bind_rule(&catalog,&catalog.tables[&w.table],&ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:e.clone(),site:e.site().clone()}).ok()).filter(super::operation::safe).collect());
            let has_role=kernel::dependencies(&safe).iter().any(|dep|!super::privilege::role_columns(bound,dep).is_empty());
            let relationship=conditions(&w.context).iter().any(|e|{let text=crate::print::expression(e);text.contains("viewer")&&text.contains('.')});
            if has_role&&!relationship&&seen.insert((w.p.name.clone(),"wide-write".into())){note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE",format!("{}: may any admitted role write any {} row and record themselves? Does the spec mean owner of THIS resource?",w.p.name,w.table),"model the resource-to-owner principal/reference relationship and require it for this row; a global owner role is not ownership of every resource");}
        }
        // A grant's stored recipient, not its assigner, must have the role.
        if creating||w.op==WriteOp::Update{
            for f in &w.fields{if !matches!(t.columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Principal)){continue;}
                let Some(Expr::Name{name:recipient,..})=field_value(f)else{continue;};
                if recipient=="viewer"||!w.p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*recipient&&matches!(a.type_.kind,TypeKind::Principal)){continue;}
                for condition in conditions(&w.context).into_iter().flat_map(conjunction){
                    let Some(role)=bound_role(&catalog,&catalog.tables[&w.table],condition,recipient)else{continue;};
                    let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,index,key,equalities,..}=&role else{continue;};
                    let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,roster);
                    if !super::lints::mutable_role(bound,roster)||!super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,roster,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|role_columns.contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_)))){continue;}
                    for (name,target) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for branch in target.rules.read.disjuncts(){
                        let trusts=positive_probes(branch).iter().any(|probe|if let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=probe{table==&w.table&&super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|c==&f.name&&*v==RuleTerm::Viewer)}else{false});
                        if trusts&&kernel::implies(branch,&role)!=Ok(true)&&seen.insert((name.clone(),format!("grant:{}:{}",w.table,role.quarry()))){note(&mut out,&Site::default(),"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT",format!("{name}.read: retained {} grants are written by {} requiring recipient `{}`, but read branch `{}` does not recheck that mutable current role",w.table,w.p.name,role.quarry(),branch.quarry()),"conjoin the current recipient role at read time, or revoke derived grants on every role change; individually acknowledge intentional retained authority");}
                    }}
                }
            }
        }
    }
    transition_notes(bound,&catalog,&writes,&mut out);
    duration_notes(&writes,&mut out);
    let mut dedup=BTreeSet::new();out.retain(|n|dedup.insert((n.code.clone(),n.message.clone())));out
}
fn positive_probes(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(positive_probes).collect(),RuleExpr::Exists{..}=>vec![rule],_=>vec![]}}
fn transition_notes(bound:&Bound,_catalog:&Catalog,writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
    for w in writes{let t=&bound.schema.tables[&w.table];
        if w.op==WriteOp::Update{for f in &w.fields{
            let Some(ColumnType::Enum{..})=t.columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required)else{continue;};
            let Some(Expr::Name{name,..})=field_value(f)else{continue;};
            let Some(arg)=w.p.args.iter().find(|a|a.name==*name)else{continue;};let TypeKind::Enum{variants}=&arg.type_.kind else{continue;};
            for other in writes.iter().filter(|o|o.op==WriteOp::Update&&o.table==w.table&&o.p.name!=w.p.name){
                let mut targets=BTreeSet::new();for g in other.fields.iter().filter(|g|g.name==f.name){if let Some(value)=field_value(g){walk(value,|e|if let Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(v),..}=e{targets.insert(v.clone());});}}
                for target in &targets{
                    if variants.contains(target)&&!conditions(&w.context).iter().any(|e|super::lints::argument_guard(e,name,target)==Some(false))&&kernel::implies(&w.gate,&other.gate)==Ok(false){note(out,&w.site,"N_TRANSITION_BYPASS",format!("{}: generic {}.{} setter includes '{target}', also set by stricter {} ({})",w.p.name,w.table,f.name,other.p.name,other.gate.quarry()),"enforce the decision's authority on every path reaching this status; declare independent transition/column update intent and run test");}
                }
            }
        }}
        if !matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
        let deps=conditions(&w.context).iter().flat_map(|e|reads(e)).filter(|dep|dep!=&w.table&&super::privilege::role_columns(bound,dep).is_empty()&&writes.iter().any(|o|&o.table==dep&&o.op!=WriteOp::Insert)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        for dep in deps{for later in writes.iter().filter(|o|o.p.name!=w.p.name&&(o.table==w.table&&o.op==WriteOp::Update||bound.schema.tables[&o.table].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Ref{table} if table==&w.table)))){
            if conditions(&later.context).iter().any(|e|reads(e).contains(&dep)){continue;}
            note(out,&later.site,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK",format!("{}: {} creation by {} checks mutable {dep}, but this later {} write does not recheck it",later.p.name,w.table,w.p.name,later.table),"if eligibility must remain current at approval/transition, recheck it there; declare independent intent and test a create -> eligibility change -> approval history");
        }}
    }
}

fn duration_number(e:&Expr)->Option<i64>{
    let Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Number(s),..}=e else{return None;};
    for (suffix,scale) in [("ms",1),("s",1000),("m",60_000),("h",3_600_000),("d",86_400_000)]{
        if let Some(n)=s.strip_suffix(suffix){return n.parse::<i64>().ok()?.checked_mul(scale);}
    }s.parse().ok()
}
fn duration_notes(writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
    let mut bounds=BTreeMap::<(String,String,String),BTreeMap<i64,BTreeSet<String>>>::new();
    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
    for w in writes{for condition in conditions(&w.context){walk(condition,|e|{
        let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=e else{return;};if !matches!(op.as_str(),">"|">="|"<"|"<="){return;}
        for (field,clock,cmp) in [(left.as_ref(),right.as_ref(),op.as_str()),(right.as_ref(),left.as_ref(),match op.as_str(){">"=>"<",">="=>"<=","<"=>">",_=>">="})]{
            let Expr::Binary{op:offset,left:now,right:amount,..}=clock else{continue;};
            if !matches!(now.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now")||!matches!(offset.as_str(),"-"|"+"){continue;}
            let Some(mut duration)=duration_number(amount)else{continue;};if offset=="-"{duration=-duration;}
            let target=match field{
                Expr::Member{base,name,..}=>reads(base).into_iter().next().map(|table|(table,name.clone())),
                Expr::Name{name,..}=>writes.iter().filter(|a|a.p.name==w.p.name).find_map(|a|a.fields.iter().find(|f|field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Name{name:n,..} if n==name))).map(|f|(a.table.clone(),f.name.clone()))),_=>None,
            };
            if let Some((table,column))=target{let key=(table,column,cmp.to_string());sites.entry(key.clone()).or_insert_with(||w.site.clone());bounds.entry(key).or_default().entry(duration).or_default().insert(w.p.name.clone());}
        }
    });}}
    for ((table,column,cmp),values) in bounds{if values.len()<2{continue;}
        let entries=values.iter().map(|(ms,ops)|format!("now {ms:+}ms in {}",ops.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ");
        note(out,&sites[&(table.clone(),column.clone(),cmp.clone())],"N_DURATION_BOUNDS",format!("{table}.{column}: inconsistent `{cmp}` duration bounds across operations: {entries}; are these different windows intentional?"),"use the specification's duration consistently at creation, renewal and approval, or document the deliberate workflow difference");
    }
}
//! Individually justified source-bound dispositions; never proof or authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — bulk compiler-note review cannot clear security gates.
use super::*;
use serde::{Serialize,Deserialize};
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
const ANALYZER:&str="sec7-2";
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct File{version:u32,entries:BTreeMap<String,Entry>}
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Entry{sources:String,analyzer:String,reason:String}
#[derive(Serialize)]
pub(super) struct Disposition{pub id:String,pub code:String,pub site:String,pub gated:bool,pub acknowledged:bool,pub reason:Option<String>}
pub(super) fn gated(code:&str)->bool{matches!(code,"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT"|"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE")}
fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String{format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
fn digest(s:&str)->bool{s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit())}
fn id(n:&snapback4_lang::Diagnostic)->String{hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::to_value(n).unwrap()).unwrap())}
pub(super) fn review(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>)->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    if project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed during security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut sorted=sources.to_vec();sorted.sort_by(|a,b|a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    let fingerprint=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&sorted).unwrap());
    let path=project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");
    let mut file:File=match std::fs::read(&path){
        Ok(bytes)=>serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_|"invalid snapback/security-reviews.json; expected version 1 with per-note source/analyzer/reason records".to_string())?,
        Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>File{version:1,entries:BTreeMap::new()},
        Err(e)=>return Err(format!("cannot read security dispositions: {e}")),
    };
    if file.version!=1||file.entries.iter().any(|(key,e)|!digest(key)||!digest(&e.sources)||e.reason.trim().is_empty()||e.analyzer.is_empty()){return Err("invalid security disposition version/digest/reason".into());}
    if let Some((key,reason))=ack{
        if reason.trim().is_empty(){return Err("--reason needs the specification rationale for this individual note".into());}
        if !notes.iter().any(|n|id(n)==key){return Err("--ack-security ID is not a current security note; run check --json".into());}
        file.entries.insert(key.into(),Entry{sources:fingerprint.clone(),analyzer:ANALYZER.into(),reason:reason.trim().into()});
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let result=std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).unwrap()).and_then(|_|std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path));
        if let Err(e)=result{let _=std::fs::remove_file(&temporary);return Err(format!("cannot record security disposition: {e}"));}
    }
    Ok(notes.iter().map(|n|{let key=id(n);let entry=file.entries.get(&key).filter(|e|e.sources==fingerprint&&e.analyzer==ANALYZER);
        Disposition{id:key,code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:gated(&n.code),acknowledged:entry.is_some(),reason:entry.map(|e|e.reason.clone())}
    }).collect())
}
pub(super) fn pending(dispositions:&[Disposition])->bool{dispositions.iter().any(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged)}
pub(super) fn print(dispositions:&[Disposition]){for d in dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.gated||d.acknowledged){
    if d.acknowledged{println!("security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));}
    else{println!("security review required {} {} at {}; repair or check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",d.id,d.code,d.site,d.id);}
}}
#[cfg(test)]mod tests{
use super::*;
#[test]fn individual_source_and_diagnostic_bound_dispositions(){
 let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec7-dispositions-{}",std::process::id()));std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();
 let p=Project{root:root.clone()};std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return true\n").unwrap();let sources=p.sources().unwrap();
 let a=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","a","");let b=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT","b","");let notes=[a.clone(),b];
 assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&id(&a),"  "))).is_err());assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some(("unknown","intended"))).is_err());
 let ds=review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&id(&a),"the specification allows optional draft evidence"))).unwrap();assert!(ds[0].acknowledged);assert!(!ds[1].acknowledged);assert!(pending(&ds));
 let changed=[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","changed","")];assert!(!review(&p,&sources,&changed,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 let path=p.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");let mut f:File=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(&path).unwrap()).unwrap();f.entries.get_mut(&id(&a)).unwrap().analyzer="old".into();std::fs::write(&path,serde_json::to_vec(&f).unwrap()).unwrap();assert!(!review(&p,&sources,&notes,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return false\n").unwrap();assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&id(&a),"intended"))).is_err());assert!(!review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 std::fs::write(&path,"{}").unwrap();assert!(review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).is_err());std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
}
}

    27	    for p in docs.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation){
    28	        for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
    29	            let Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=write else{continue;};
    30	            let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&table)else{continue;};
    31	            let mut requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context);
    32	            for condition in conditions(&context){let mut value=condition.clone();
    33	                super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |e|if let Expr::Member{base,..}=e&&reads(base).contains(&table){*base=Box::new(Expr::Focal{site:site.clone()});});
    34	                if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(&catalog,info,&ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value,site:site.clone()}){requirements.push(rule);}
    35	            }
    36	            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];let rule=if op==WriteOp::Update{t.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else{t.rules.insert.clone()};
    37	            writes.push(Write{p,op,table,fields,site,context,gate:RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([rule]).collect())});
    38	        }
    39	    }
    40	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
    41	    for w in &writes{
    42	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&w.table];let creating=matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert);
    43	        if creating{
    44	            // Only unconditional requirements before the FIRST creating write
    45	            // establish mandatory parent evidence; a conditional child's guard cannot.
    46	            let first=writes.iter().find(|a|std::ptr::eq(a.p,w.p)&&matches!(a.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert));
    47	            for f in &w.fields{let Some(value)=field_value(f)else{continue;};let Some(kind)=t.columns.get(&f.name)else{continue;};
    48	                for arg in &w.p.args{if !uses(value,&arg.name){continue;}
    49	                    if arg.type_.optional&&matches!(arg.type_.kind,TypeKind::Image{..}|TypeKind::File{..})&&kind.contains_asset()&&!first.is_some_and(|a|nonnull(&a.context,&arg.name))&&seen.insert((w.p.name.clone(),format!("upload:{}",arg.name))){
    50	                        note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE",format!("{}: optional {} can omit media while creating {}; including conditional child evidence, does the spec require this upload?",w.p.name,arg.name,w.table),"make the creating mutation's upload argument required, or require it before creating the parent; intentional optionality needs an individual security disposition");
    51	                    }
    52	                    if f.name.to_lowercase().contains("reason")&&matches!(arg.type_.kind,TypeKind::Text{min:None|Some(0),..})&&seen.insert((w.p.name.clone(),format!("reason:{}",f.name))){
    53	                        note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_CREATION_REASON",format!("{}: {}.{} accepts an empty/whitespace reason at creation",w.p.name,w.table,f.name),"if the workflow requires justification, use text 1..N (non-whitespace minimum), including on optional reasons when supplied");
    54	                    }
    55	                    let money=matches!(kind.required(),ColumnType::Money{..})||["price","charge","fee","amount"].iter().any(|word|f.name.to_lowercase().contains(word))&&matches!(kind.required(),ColumnType::Int|ColumnType::IntRange{..}|ColumnType::Decimal{..});
    56	                    if money&&arg.type_.optional&&kind.is_optional()&&seen.insert((w.p.name.clone(),format!("money:{}",f.name))){note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_CREATION_MONEY",format!("{}: {}.{} permits omitted money at creation; is the required amount recorded?",w.p.name,w.table,f.name),"model the amount and require it at the workflow boundary when the spec requires payment or a price");}
    57	                }
    58	                if matches!(value,Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Bool(true),..})&&["paid","approv","payment"].iter().any(|s|f.name.to_lowercase().contains(s)){
    59	                    note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_CONSTANT_APPROVAL",format!("{}: {}.{} is stored as constant true; what verifies the payment/approval and its amount? (name-based question)",w.p.name,w.table,f.name),"record the required money/evidence and independently authorized decision; confirm constant flags against the spec");
    60	                }
    61	            }
    62	        }
    63	        // A global role plus viewer attribution does not model ownership of this resource.
    64	        if w.op==WriteOp::Update&&w.fields.iter().any(|f|matches!(field_value(f),Some(Expr::Name{name,..}) if name=="viewer")&&matches!(t.columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Principal))){
    65	            let safe=RuleExpr::And(conditions(&w.context).into_iter().filter_map(|e|super::super::schema::bind_rule(&catalog,&catalog.tables[&w.table],&ast::RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:e.clone(),site:e.site().clone()}).ok()).filter(super::operation::safe).collect());
    66	            let has_role=kernel::dependencies(&safe).iter().any(|dep|!super::privilege::role_columns(bound,dep).is_empty());
    67	            let relationship=conditions(&w.context).iter().any(|e|{let text=crate::print::expression(e);text.contains("viewer")&&text.contains('.')});
    68	            if has_role&&!relationship&&seen.insert((w.p.name.clone(),"wide-write".into())){note(&mut out,&w.site,"N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE",format!("{}: may any admitted role write any {} row and record themselves? Does the spec mean owner of THIS resource?",w.p.name,w.table),"model the resource-to-owner principal/reference relationship and require it for this row; a global owner role is not ownership of every resource");}
    69	        }
    70	        // A grant's stored recipient, not its assigner, must have the role.
    71	        if creating||w.op==WriteOp::Update{
    72	            for f in &w.fields{if !matches!(t.columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required),Some(ColumnType::Principal)){continue;}
    73	                let Some(Expr::Name{name:recipient,..})=field_value(f)else{continue;};
    74	                if recipient=="viewer"||!w.p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*recipient&&matches!(a.type_.kind,TypeKind::Principal)){continue;}
    75	                for condition in conditions(&w.context).into_iter().flat_map(conjunction){
    76	                    let Some(role)=bound_role(&catalog,&catalog.tables[&w.table],condition,recipient)else{continue;};
    77	                    let RuleExpr::Exists{table:roster,index,key,equalities,..}=&role else{continue;};
    78	                    let role_columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,roster);
    79	                    if !super::lints::mutable_role(bound,roster)||!super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,roster,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|role_columns.contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_)))){continue;}
    80	                    for (name,target) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for branch in target.rules.read.disjuncts(){
    81	                        let trusts=positive_probes(branch).iter().any(|probe|if let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=probe{table==&w.table&&super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|c==&f.name&&*v==RuleTerm::Viewer)}else{false});
    82	                        if trusts&&kernel::implies(branch,&role)!=Ok(true)&&seen.insert((name.clone(),format!("grant:{}:{}",w.table,role.quarry()))){note(&mut out,&Site::default(),"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT",format!("{name}.read: retained {} grants are written by {} requiring recipient `{}`, but read branch `{}` does not recheck that mutable current role",w.table,w.p.name,role.quarry(),branch.quarry()),"conjoin the current recipient role at read time, or revoke derived grants on every role change; individually acknowledge intentional retained authority");}
    83	                    }}
    84	                }
    85	            }
    86	        }
    87	    }
    88	    transition_notes(bound,&catalog,&writes,&mut out);
    89	    duration_notes(&writes,&mut out);
    90	    let mut dedup=BTreeSet::new();out.retain(|n|dedup.insert((n.code.clone(),n.message.clone())));out
    91	}
    92	fn positive_probes(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(positive_probes).collect(),RuleExpr::Exists{..}=>vec![rule],_=>vec![]}}
    93	fn transition_notes(bound:&Bound,_catalog:&Catalog,writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
    94	    for w in writes{let t=&bound.schema.tables[&w.table];
    95	        if w.op==WriteOp::Update{for f in &w.fields{
    96	            let Some(ColumnType::Enum{..})=t.columns.get(&f.name).map(ColumnType::required)else{continue;};
    97	            let Some(Expr::Name{name,..})=field_value(f)else{continue;};
    98	            let Some(arg)=w.p.args.iter().find(|a|a.name==*name)else{continue;};let TypeKind::Enum{variants}=&arg.type_.kind else{continue;};
    99	            for other in writes.iter().filter(|o|o.op==WriteOp::Update&&o.table==w.table&&o.p.name!=w.p.name){
   100	                let mut targets=BTreeSet::new();for g in other.fields.iter().filter(|g|g.name==f.name){if let Some(value)=field_value(g){walk(value,|e|if let Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(v),..}=e{targets.insert(v.clone());});}}
   101	                for target in &targets{
   102	                    if variants.contains(target)&&!conditions(&w.context).iter().any(|e|super::lints::argument_guard(e,name,target)==Some(false))&&kernel::implies(&w.gate,&other.gate)==Ok(false){note(out,&w.site,"N_TRANSITION_BYPASS",format!("{}: generic {}.{} setter includes '{target}', also set by stricter {} ({})",w.p.name,w.table,f.name,other.p.name,other.gate.quarry()),"enforce the decision's authority on every path reaching this status; declare independent transition/column update intent and run test");}
   103	                }
   104	            }
   105	        }}
   106	        if !matches!(w.op,WriteOp::Insert|WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
   107	        let deps=conditions(&w.context).iter().flat_map(|e|reads(e)).filter(|dep|dep!=&w.table&&super::privilege::role_columns(bound,dep).is_empty()&&writes.iter().any(|o|&o.table==dep&&o.op!=WriteOp::Insert)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   108	        for dep in deps{for later in writes.iter().filter(|o|o.p.name!=w.p.name&&(o.table==w.table&&o.op==WriteOp::Update||bound.schema.tables[&o.table].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Ref{table} if table==&w.table)))){
   109	            if conditions(&later.context).iter().any(|e|reads(e).contains(&dep)){continue;}
   110	            note(out,&later.site,"N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK",format!("{}: {} creation by {} checks mutable {dep}, but this later {} write does not recheck it",later.p.name,w.table,w.p.name,later.table),"if eligibility must remain current at approval/transition, recheck it there; declare independent intent and test a create -> eligibility change -> approval history");
   111	        }}
   112	    }
   113	}
   114	
   115	fn duration_number(e:&Expr)->Option<i64>{
   116	    let Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Number(s),..}=e else{return None;};
   117	    for (suffix,scale) in [("ms",1),("s",1000),("m",60_000),("h",3_600_000),("d",86_400_000)]{
   118	        if let Some(n)=s.strip_suffix(suffix){return n.parse::<i64>().ok()?.checked_mul(scale);}
   119	    }s.parse().ok()
   120	}
   121	fn duration_notes(writes:&[Write<'_>],out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>){
   122	    let mut bounds=BTreeMap::<(String,String,String),BTreeMap<i64,BTreeSet<String>>>::new();
   123	    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
   124	    for w in writes{for condition in conditions(&w.context){walk(condition,|e|{
   125	        let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=e else{return;};if !matches!(op.as_str(),">"|">="|"<"|"<="){return;}
   126	        for (field,clock,cmp) in [(left.as_ref(),right.as_ref(),op.as_str()),(right.as_ref(),left.as_ref(),match op.as_str(){">"=>"<",">="=>"<=","<"=>">",_=>">="})]{
   127	            let Expr::Binary{op:offset,left:now,right:amount,..}=clock else{continue;};
   128	            if !matches!(now.as_ref(),Expr::Name{name,..} if name=="now")||!matches!(offset.as_str(),"-"|"+"){continue;}
   129	            let Some(mut duration)=duration_number(amount)else{continue;};if offset=="-"{duration=-duration;}
   130	            let target=match field{
   131	                Expr::Member{base,name,..}=>reads(base).into_iter().next().map(|table|(table,name.clone())),
   132	                Expr::Name{name,..}=>writes.iter().filter(|a|a.p.name==w.p.name).find_map(|a|a.fields.iter().find(|f|field_value(f).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,Expr::Name{name:n,..} if n==name))).map(|f|(a.table.clone(),f.name.clone()))),_=>None,
   133	            };
   134	            if let Some((table,column))=target{let key=(table,column,cmp.to_string());sites.entry(key.clone()).or_insert_with(||w.site.clone());bounds.entry(key).or_default().entry(duration).or_default().insert(w.p.name.clone());}
   135	        }
   136	    });}}
   137	    for ((table,column,cmp),values) in bounds{if values.len()<2{continue;}
   138	        let entries=values.iter().map(|(ms,ops)|format!("now {ms:+}ms in {}",ops.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ");
   139	        note(out,&sites[&(table.clone(),column.clone(),cmp.clone())],"N_DURATION_BOUNDS",format!("{table}.{column}: inconsistent `{cmp}` duration bounds across operations: {entries}; are these different windows intentional?"),"use the specification's duration consistently at creation, renewal and approval, or document the deliberate workflow difference");
   140	    }
   141	}

     7	#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
     8	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
     9	struct File{version:u32,entries:BTreeMap<String,Entry>}
    10	#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
    11	#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
    12	struct Entry{sources:String,analyzer:String,reason:String}
    13	#[derive(Serialize)]
    14	pub(super) struct Disposition{pub id:String,pub code:String,pub site:String,pub gated:bool,pub acknowledged:bool,pub reason:Option<String>}
    15	pub(super) fn gated(code:&str)->bool{matches!(code,"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT"|"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE")}

    17	fn digest(s:&str)->bool{s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit())}
    18	fn id(n:&snapback4_lang::Diagnostic)->String{hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::to_value(n).unwrap()).unwrap())}
    19	pub(super) fn review(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>)->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    20	    if project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed during security review; rerun check".into());}
    21	    let mut sorted=sources.to_vec();sorted.sort_by(|a,b|a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    22	    let fingerprint=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&sorted).unwrap());
    23	    let path=project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");
    24	    let mut file:File=match std::fs::read(&path){
    25	        Ok(bytes)=>serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_|"invalid snapback/security-reviews.json; expected version 1 with per-note source/analyzer/reason records".to_string())?,
    26	        Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>File{version:1,entries:BTreeMap::new()},
    27	        Err(e)=>return Err(format!("cannot read security dispositions: {e}")),
    28	    };
    29	    if file.version!=1||file.entries.iter().any(|(key,e)|!digest(key)||!digest(&e.sources)||e.reason.trim().is_empty()||e.analyzer.is_empty()){return Err("invalid security disposition version/digest/reason".into());}
    30	    if let Some((key,reason))=ack{
    31	        if reason.trim().is_empty(){return Err("--reason needs the specification rationale for this individual note".into());}
    32	        if !notes.iter().any(|n|id(n)==key){return Err("--ack-security ID is not a current security note; run check --json".into());}
    33	        file.entries.insert(key.into(),Entry{sources:fingerprint.clone(),analyzer:ANALYZER.into(),reason:reason.trim().into()});
    34	        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
    35	        let result=std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).unwrap()).and_then(|_|std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path));
    36	        if let Err(e)=result{let _=std::fs::remove_file(&temporary);return Err(format!("cannot record security disposition: {e}"));}
    37	    }
    38	    Ok(notes.iter().map(|n|{let key=id(n);let entry=file.entries.get(&key).filter(|e|e.sources==fingerprint&&e.analyzer==ANALYZER);
    39	        Disposition{id:key,code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:gated(&n.code),acknowledged:entry.is_some(),reason:entry.map(|e|e.reason.clone())}
    40	    }).collect())
    41	}
    42	pub(super) fn pending(dispositions:&[Disposition])->bool{dispositions.iter().any(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged)}
    43	pub(super) fn print(dispositions:&[Disposition]){for d in dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.gated||d.acknowledged){
    44	    if d.acknowledged{println!("security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));}
    45	    else{println!("security review required {} {} at {}; repair or check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",d.id,d.code,d.site,d.id);}
    46	}}

//! Public check gates are individual dispositions, independent of proof verdicts.
use std::{fs,path::PathBuf,process::Command};
use serde_json::Value;
struct Scratch(PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
fn scratch()->Scratch{let p=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec7-cli-{}",std::process::id()));fs::create_dir_all(p.join("snapback")).unwrap();Scratch(p)}
const SOURCE:&str="table docs:\n  photo: image <=10000 ?\n  other: image <=10000 ?\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(photo: image <=10000 ?, other: image <=10000 ?):\n  return insert docs {photo,other}\n";
fn check(p:&Scratch,args:&[&str])->(i32,Value){let r=Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_snapback4")).args(["check","--json"]).args(args).current_dir(&p.0).output().unwrap();let j=serde_json::from_slice(&r.stdout).unwrap_or_else(|_|panic!("{}",String::from_utf8_lossy(&r.stdout)));(r.status.code().unwrap_or(-1),j)}
#[test]fn one_note_at_a_time_bulk_cannot_clear_and_source_edits_invalidate(){
 let p=scratch();let source=p.0.join("snapback/schema.q");fs::write(&source,SOURCE).unwrap();
 let (code,j)=check(&p,&[]);assert_eq!(code,1);assert_eq!(j["ok"],false);assert!(j["security"]["invariants"].as_array().unwrap().is_empty());
 assert_eq!(j["securityReviews"],check(&p,&[]).1["securityReviews"],"same input must produce stable IDs");
 let pending=j["securityReviews"].as_array().unwrap().iter().filter(|d|d["gated"]==true).map(|d|d["id"].as_str().unwrap().to_owned()).collect::<Vec<_>>();assert_eq!(pending.len(),2);
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-notes"]).0,1);assert!(!p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json").exists());
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","optional draft photo per specification"]).0,1);
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[1],"--reason","optional second draft photo per specification"]).0,0);
 let review=p.0.join("snapback/security-reviews.json");let bytes=fs::read(&review).unwrap();
 fs::write(&source,format!("{SOURCE}\n-- source changed\n")).unwrap();assert_eq!(check(&p,&[]).0,1);
 assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","  "]).0,2);assert_eq!(fs::read(&review).unwrap(),bytes);
 fs::write(&source,"not valid Quarry\n").unwrap();assert_eq!(check(&p,&["--ack-security",&pending[0],"--reason","cannot override compile error"]).0,1);assert_eq!(fs::read(review).unwrap(),bytes);
}

use snapback4_lang::{compile,security::{self,challenges::{self,Campaign}}};
fn bound(s:&str)->snapback4_lang::Bound{let b=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),s.into())]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);b}
fn notes(s:&str)->security::Report{let b=bound(s);let mut r=security::analyze_rules(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut r,&[("schema.q".into(),s.into())],&b);r}

 (include_str!("security/wave22/044.q"),vec!["N_CONSTANT_APPROVAL","N_DURATION_BOUNDS"]),
 ]{let s=source.lines().filter(|l|!l.starts_with("invariant ")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");let r=notes(&s);
 for code in codes{assert!(r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code==code&&n.site.line>0),"missing {code}: {:?}",r.notes);}}
}

#[test]fn optional_upload_guard_and_alias_controls(){

 }

}

//! Expand advisory write sites with lexical arguments and dominating guards.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#128-runtime-intent-and-independent-worlds — helper placement cannot hide a grant writer.
use super::*;
use ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramDecl,FunctionBody};
type Env=BTreeMap<String,Expr>;
fn resolve(expr:&Expr,env:&Env)->Expr{
    let mut expr=expr.clone();
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|{
        if let Expr::Object{fields,..}|Expr::Write{fields,..}=e{for f in fields{if f.value.is_none(){f.value=Some(env.get(&f.name).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name{name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone()}));}}}
        if let Expr::Name{name,..}=e&&let Some(value)=env.get(name){*e=value.clone();}
    });expr
}
fn expression(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,guards:&[Stmt],depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    let mut expr=resolve(expr,env);
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();out.push((e.clone(),context));},
        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
            match &f.body{
                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,depth+1,out),
                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,depth+1,out),
            }
        },_=>{}
    });
}
// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
    }}out
}
fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    for s in body{match s{
        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
        },
        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);break;},
        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,site}=>{
            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,depth,out);
            let condition=resolve(condition,env);
            let mut then_guards=guards.clone();
            then_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:condition.clone(),code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut then_guards,depth,out);
            let mut else_guards=guards.clone();
            else_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:Expr::Unary{op:"not".into(),value:Box::new(condition),site:site.clone()},code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut else_guards,depth,out);
        },
        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),depth,out);},
        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),depth,out),_=>{}
    }}
}
pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();
    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);
    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,0,&mut out);out
}
//! Creation is a write even when an edit operation has the stronger guard.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#151-every-executed-write — conditional advice never infers creation policy.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|crate::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    let mut writes=Vec::new();
    for doc in &documents{for p in &doc.programs{if p.kind!=ast::ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
        for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
            let ast::Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=write else{continue;};
            let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&table)else{continue;};
            let requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context);
            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
            let gate=if op==ast::WriteOp::Update{t.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else{t.rules.insert.clone()};
            let admission=RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([gate]).collect());
            writes.push((p.name.clone(),op,table,fields,site,admission));
        }
    }}
    fn necessary(rule:&RuleExpr)->RuleExpr{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(necessary).collect()),
        _ if super::operation::safe(rule)=>rule.clone(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow,
    }}
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();
    for (name,op,table,fields,site,admission) in &writes{
        if !matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
        for (editor,edit,other,patch,_,restricted) in &writes{
            if *edit!=ast::WriteOp::Update||other!=table{continue;}
            let restriction=necessary(restricted);
            if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&restriction)==Ok(true)||kernel::implies(admission,&restriction)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let columns=patch.iter().filter(|f|fields.iter().any(|insert|{
                if insert.name!=f.name{return false;}
                let Some(value)=&insert.value else{return true;};
                let mut value=value.clone();let mut supplied=false;
                super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |e|if let ast::Expr::Name{name:argument,..}=e{
                    supplied|=documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.name==*name).any(|p|p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*argument));
                });supplied
            })).map(|f|f.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if columns.is_empty()||!seen.insert((name.clone(),table.clone(),columns.clone(),restriction.quarry())){continue;}
            out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,"N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT",format!("{name}: {table}.{} can be supplied at insert/upsert without the edit restriction of {editor}: {}; creation admission: {}",columns.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","),restriction.quarry(),admission.quarry()),"confirm whether the spec permits different creation authority; otherwise guard every insert/upsert arm too and declare column write only intent, then run snapback4 test").guide("security"));
        }
    }out
}
//! Advisory lineage through compiled result/refusal expressions, never a proof.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#152-mutation-results-are-observations — projection is one observer, not a privacy boundary.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr,Stmt};
type Sources=BTreeSet<(String,String)>;
#[derive(Clone,Default)]
struct Flow{sources:Sources,fields:BTreeMap<String,Flow>}
impl Flow{
    fn all(&self)->Sources{self.sources.iter().cloned().chain(self.fields.values().flat_map(Flow::all)).collect()}
    fn union(xs:impl IntoIterator<Item=Flow>)->Self{Self{sources:xs.into_iter().flat_map(|f|f.all()).collect(),fields:BTreeMap::new()}}
    fn join(mut a:Flow,b:Flow)->Flow{a.sources.extend(b.sources);for(n,v)in b.fields{let old=a.fields.remove(&n).unwrap_or_default();a.fields.insert(n,Self::join(old,v));}a}
}
fn row(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->Flow{Flow{sources:Sources::new(),fields:bound.schema.tables.get(table).map(|t|t.columns.keys().map(|c|(c.clone(),Flow{sources:BTreeSet::from([(table.into(),c.clone())]),fields:BTreeMap::new()})).collect()).unwrap_or_default()}}
fn value(bound:&Bound,e:&Expr,env:&mut BTreeMap<String,Flow>,reads:&mut BTreeSet<String>,returned:&mut Sources,refusals:&mut Sources)->Flow{match e{
    Expr::Variable(n)=>env.get(n).cloned().unwrap_or_default(),
    Expr::Field{base,name}=>{let f=value(bound,base,env,reads,returned,refusals);f.fields.get(name).cloned().unwrap_or(Flow{sources:f.sources,fields:BTreeMap::new()})},
    Expr::Object(fields)=>Flow{sources:Sources::new(),fields:fields.iter().map(|(n,v)|(n.clone(),value(bound,v,env,reads,returned,refusals))).collect()},
    Expr::Shape{base,fields}=>{let _=value(bound,base,env,reads,returned,refusals);Flow{sources:Sources::new(),fields:fields.iter().map(|(n,v)|(n.clone(),value(bound,v,env,reads,returned,refusals))).collect()}},
    Expr::Get(r)=>{reads.insert(r.table.clone());row(bound,&r.table)},
    Expr::Scan(s)=>{reads.insert(s.table.clone());row(bound,&s.table)},
    Expr::Merge(m)=>{reads.insert(m.table().into());row(bound,m.table())},
    Expr::Array(xs)|Expr::Builtin{args:xs,..}=>{
        // ProjectFields is the engine's column gate, retaining row identity.
        if let Expr::Builtin{builtin:ir::Builtin::ProjectFields{table},..}=e{
            reads.insert(table.clone());return row(bound,table);
        }
        if let Expr::Builtin{builtin:ir::Builtin::OptionalField{field,..},..}=e{
            let f=value(bound,&xs[0],env,reads,returned,refusals);return f.fields.get(field).cloned().unwrap_or(Flow{sources:f.sources,fields:BTreeMap::new()});
        }
        if matches!(e,Expr::Builtin{builtin:ir::Builtin::ProjectFields{..}|ir::Builtin::RequireUncapped|ir::Builtin::First|ir::Builtin::Last|ir::Builtin::At|ir::Builtin::Sort{..},..})&&!xs.is_empty(){return value(bound,&xs[0],env,reads,returned,refusals);}
        Flow::union(xs.iter().map(|e|value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals)))
    },
    Expr::Binary{left,right,..}=>{
        let a=value(bound,left,env,reads,returned,refusals);let b=value(bound,right,env,reads,returned,refusals);
        if matches!(&**left,Expr::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))&&!b.fields.is_empty()||matches!(&**right,Expr::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Null))&&!a.fields.is_empty(){Flow::default()}else{Flow::union([a,b])}
    },
    Expr::Unary{value:e,..}=>value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals),
    Expr::Block{body,value:e}=>{statements(bound,body,env,reads,returned,refusals);value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals)},
    _=>Flow::default(),
}}
fn statements(bound:&Bound,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut BTreeMap<String,Flow>,reads:&mut BTreeSet<String>,returned:&mut Sources,refusals:&mut Sources){for s in body{match s{
    Stmt::Const{name,value:e}|Stmt::Let{name,value:e}|Stmt::Assign{name,value:e}=>{let f=value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals);env.insert(name.clone(),f);},
    Stmt::Push{target,value:e}=>{let f=value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals);let old=env.remove(target).unwrap_or_default();env.insert(target.clone(),Flow::union([old,f]));},
    Stmt::Insert{table,row:Expr::Variable(n)}|Stmt::InsertIfAbsent{table,row:Expr::Variable(n),..}=>{env.insert(n.clone(),row(bound,table));},
    Stmt::WriteTarget{target,result:Some(n),..}=>{reads.insert(target.table.clone());env.insert(n.clone(),row(bound,&target.table));},
    Stmt::Return(e)=>{returned.extend(value(bound,e,env,reads,&mut Sources::new(),refusals).all());},
    Stmt::Require{condition,..}|Stmt::Check{condition,..}=>{refusals.extend(value(bound,condition,env,reads,returned,&mut Sources::new()).all());},
    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body}=>{
        let condition=value(bound,condition,env,reads,returned,refusals).all();
        // A private branch can choose both results and authored refusals.
        returned.extend(condition.iter().cloned());refusals.extend(condition);
        let mut other=env.clone();statements(bound,then_body,env,reads,returned,refusals);statements(bound,else_body,&mut other,reads,returned,refusals);
        for (n,f) in other{let old=env.remove(&n).unwrap_or_default();env.insert(n,Flow::join(old,f));}
    },
    Stmt::For{binding,domain,body}=>{match domain{
        ir::LoopDomain::Page(s)=>{reads.insert(s.table.clone());env.insert(binding.clone(),row(bound,&s.table));},
        ir::LoopDomain::DerivedArray{value:e,..}=>{let f=value(bound,e,env,reads,returned,refusals);env.insert(binding.clone(),f);},_=>{},
    }statements(bound,body,env,reads,returned,refusals);},_=>{},
}}}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let flows=bound.programs.iter().map(|bp|{let(mut reads,mut returned,mut refusals)=(BTreeSet::new(),Sources::new(),Sources::new());statements(bound,&bp.program.body,&mut BTreeMap::new(),&mut reads,&mut returned,&mut refusals);(bp,reads,returned,refusals)}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut hidden:BTreeMap<(String,String),BTreeSet<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
    for (bp,reads,returned,_) in &flows{if bp.program.kind!=ir::ProgramKind::Query{continue;}
        for table in reads{if let Some(t)=bound.schema.tables.get(table){for c in t.columns.keys().filter(|c|c.as_str()!="id"){
            if !returned.contains(&(table.clone(),c.clone())){hidden.entry((table.clone(),c.clone())).or_default().insert(bp.program.name.clone());}
        }}}
    }
    let mut grouped=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<(Site,String)>>::new();
    for (bp,_,returned,refusals) in flows{
        for (kind,origins) in [("return/write result",returned),("refusal/branch",refusals)]{
            let mut groups:BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>=BTreeMap::new();
            for (table,c) in origins{
                let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
                let projected=hidden.get(&(table.clone(),c.clone()));
                let ruled=t.column_reads.contains_key(&c)||t.written_by.get(&c).is_some_and(|g|!g.audience);
                if projected.is_none()&&!ruled{continue;}
                // A query's own narrowed result is not an alternate write observer.
                if bp.program.kind==ir::ProgramKind::Query&&kind=="return/write result"&&projected.is_none(){continue;}
                let reason=projected.map(|qs|format!("omitted by {}",qs.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))).unwrap_or_else(||"column-ruled".into());
                groups.entry(table).or_default().push(format!("{c} ({reason})"));
            }
            for (table,columns) in groups{grouped.entry(table).or_default().push((bp.site.clone(),format!("{} at {}: {kind} observes {}",bp.program.name,bp.site,columns.join(", "))));}
        }
    }
    grouped.into_iter().map(|(table,mut paths)|{
        paths.sort_by_key(|(site,_)|(site.file.clone(),site.line,site.col));
        Diagnostic::new(&paths[0].0,"N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE",format!("{table}: {}; row audience: {}",paths.iter().map(|(_,text)|text.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; "),bound.schema.tables[&table].rules.read.quarry()),"confirm each operation's result/refusal audience from the spec; a query projection alone is not privacy. Use column read rules and independent intent; run snapback4 test").guide("security")
    }).collect()
}
//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
pub(super) fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}

 pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
     if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let args=&p.args;
    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
            }
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    }
    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            let valid=match (k,literal){
                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
            };
            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
        }
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
        }}
        cases=next;
    }
    let focused=super::executed::write_arguments(bound,p).into_iter().filter(|a|matches!(p.args.get(a),Some(ir::ArgType::Optional(_)))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if focused.is_empty(){return cases;}
    let mut prioritized=Vec::new();
    for case in cases{
        if !prioritized.contains(&case){prioritized.push(case.clone());}
        for name in &focused{if let Some(value)=arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.clone(),p.args[name].clone())]),4).and_then(|mut a|a.remove(name))&&value!=Value::Null{
            let mut alternate=case.clone();alternate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !prioritized.contains(&alternate){prioritized.push(alternate);}
        }}
        if prioritized.len()>=LIMIT{prioritized.truncate(LIMIT);out.unsupported.insert("column-write argument frontier capped at 512; remaining combinations uncovered".into());break;}
    }prioritized
}
pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
pub(super) fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
}
pub(super) fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    typed_disclosure_image(ty,data,table,key,column,physical,None)
}
pub(super) fn typed_disclosure_image(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value,policy:Option<&RuleExpr>)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)))&&policy.is_none_or(|p|!p.any_term(&|term|{
        let column=match term{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::OldField(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c)=>Some(c.as_str()),RuleTerm::Reference{column,..}|RuleTerm::ReferenceChain{column,..}=>Some(column.as_str()),_=>None};
        column.filter(|c|*c!="$security:server-clock").is_some_and(|c|data.field(c).is_err()||data.field(c).ok()!=physical.field(c).ok())
    }));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
        Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)=>typed_disclosure_image(inner,data,table,key,column,physical,policy),
        Ty::Page(t,_) if t==table=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(payload)),
        Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>matches!(data,Value::Array(xs) if xs.iter().any(|r|typed_disclosure_image(inner,r,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
        Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.iter().any(|(name,ty)|data.field(name).ok().is_some_and(|v|typed_disclosure_image(ty,v,table,key,column,physical,policy))),
        _=>false,
    }
}
pub(super) fn typed_source(ty:&crate::types::Ty,table:&str)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    match ty{Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t)|Ty::Page(t,_)=>t==table,Ty::Optional(inner)|Ty::Omittable(inner)|Ty::List(inner,_)|Ty::Collection(inner,_)=>typed_source(inner,table),Ty::Object(fields)|Ty::RowShape(fields)|Ty::RowObject(fields)=>fields.values().any(|ty|typed_source(ty,table)),_=>false}
}
pub(super) fn observer_tables(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([table.into()]);
    loop{let before=out.len();
        for m in &bound.schema.maintains{if out.contains(&m.source){out.insert(m.target.clone());if let Some(owner)=&m.private{out.insert(owner.table.clone());}}}
        for (name,t) in &bound.schema.tables{if t.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|edge|out.contains(&edge.parent))||super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).iter().any(|t|out.contains(t))){out.insert(name.clone());}}
        if out.len()==before{return out;}
    }
}
fn reserve_observers(out:&mut Campaign,calls:usize)->bool{
    if !out.reserve(calls){return false;}
    if out.history_observer_calls+calls>32768{out.unsupported.insert("history read observers capped at 32768 calls; remaining disclosure combinations not covered".into());false}else{out.history_observer_calls+=calls;true}
}
fn observe(bound:&Bound,e:&mut Engine,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],steps:&[Step],out:&mut Campaign){
    if !reserve_observers(out,0)||out.history_observer_calls>=32768{return;}
    let queries=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Query&&!bp.program.native).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        if ["NewId","Emit","JobAs","JobQuery"].iter().any(|tag|json.contains(tag)){
            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: generated-id/opaque history observer needs disclosure provenance",bp.program.name));false
        }else{true}
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if queries.is_empty(){return;}
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"){
        let direct=inv.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::implies(&super::super::columns::admission(&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table],inv.column.as_deref()),p)==Ok(true));
        let indirect=bound.schema.maintains.iter().any(|m|m.source==inv.table)||bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|super::super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|kernel::dependencies(r).contains(&inv.table)));
        if direct&&!indirect{continue;}

        let observing=observer_tables(bound,&inv.table);
        for row in rows.get(&inv.table).into_iter().flatten(){'actors:for viewer in actors{
            if out.exhausted(){return;}
            if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==*viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){continue;}
            if !reserve_observers(out,1){return;}
            match policy_in(bound,inv,rows,viewer,None,row,e){Ok(false)=>{},Ok(true)=>continue,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: {why}",inv.target_label()));continue;}}
            let Ok(key)=row.field("id")else{continue;};
            if inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|c|bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].written_by.get(c)).is_some_and(|gate|!gate.audience&&row.field(&gate.owner).ok()!=Some(&Value::Id(viewer.clone()))){continue 'actors;}

            // A typed authored row return is already a payload witness. It does
            // not require an invalid absent world that deletes referenced data.
            for bp in queries.iter().copied().filter(|p|typed_source(&p.result,&inv.table)){
                let cases=fixture_cases(bound,&bp.program,rows,actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read-observer arguments capped at 32",bp.program.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){
                    if !reserve_observers(out,2){return;}
                    let args=match materialize_arguments(e,&bp.program.args,&args,viewer){Ok(a)=>a,Err(why)=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed history observer asset/input preparation: {why}",bp.program.name));continue;}};
                    out.attempted+=1;
                    if let Ok(q)=e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW))&&!q.unavailable&&typed_disclosure(&bp.result,&q.data,&inv.table,key,inv.column.as_deref(),row){
                        out.attempted+=1;
                        if !matches!(e.query(&bp.program.name,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW)),Ok(again) if !again.unavailable&&again.data==q.data){
                            out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: typed read payload is not repeatable on the committed image",bp.program.name));continue;
                        }
                        let added=violation(out,inv,steps,viewer,row,None,initial,if inv.column.is_some(){"column read observation"}else{"read"});

exec
