   266	// Prioritize actually admitted relationship changes rather than filling the
   267	// history cap with cross-products rejected by a recipient-role require.
   268	fn relationship_candidates<'a>(bound:&Bound,p:&'a Program,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],cases:&[BTreeMap<String,Value>],fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<(&'a Program,String,BTreeMap<String,Value>)>{
   269	    let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
   270	    if !p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))||!writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()&&bound.schema.tables[t].columns.values().any(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal))){return Vec::new();}
   271	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut attempts=0;
   272	    let Ok(mut judge)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{return candidates;};
   273	    for actor in actors{
   274	        let allowed=writes.iter().any(|(t,kind)|super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()&&initial.get(t).into_iter().flatten().any(|row|{
   275	            let table=&bound.schema.tables[t];let gates=if *kind=="insert"{vec![&table.rules.insert]}else{vec![&table.rules.insert,&table.rules.update]};
   276	            gates.into_iter().any(|gate|matches!(judge.fixture_policy(gate,actor,Some(row),row,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Readable)))
   277	        }));
   278	        if !allowed{continue;}
   279	        for args in cases{
   280	            if attempts>=128||candidates.len()>=16||!out.reserve(1){return candidates;}attempts+=1;
   281	            let Ok(mut e)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{continue;};
   282	            let Ok(materialized)=materialize_arguments(&mut e,&p.args,args,actor)else{continue;};
   283	            if !matches!(e.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,materialized).at(NOW)),Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})){continue;}
   284	            let Ok(after)=readback(&e)else{continue;};
   285	            if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|initial.get(t).into_iter().flatten().any(|old|after.get(t).into_iter().flatten().find(|r|r.field("id").ok()==old.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|new|bound.schema.tables[t].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&old.field(c).ok()!=new.field(c).ok())))){
   286	                candidates.push((p,actor.clone(),args.clone()));
   287	            }
   288	        }
   289	    }candidates
   290	}
   291	pub(super) fn initial_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   292	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
   293	    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
   294	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   295	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   296	    let Ok(mut engine)=fork(bound,fixture,Vec::new())else{return;};
   297	    observe(bound,&mut engine,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   298	}
   299	// Prefer real lifecycle successors before the general diagonal history matrix.
   300	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#163-directed-finite-histories — pruning orders finite replay, never proves safety.
   301	pub(super) fn lifecycle_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   302	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted()||bound.invariants.iter().all(|i|out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==i.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){return;}
   303	    let interesting=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|{
   304	        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&i.table];let gate=match i.target.as_str(){"read"=>super::super::columns::admission(t,i.column.as_deref()),"insert"=>t.rules.insert.clone(),"update"=>t.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow};
   305	        i.policy.as_ref().is_none_or(|policy|kernel::implies(&gate,policy)!=Ok(true))
   306	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   307	    if interesting.is_empty(){return;}
   308	    let mut relevant=interesting.into_iter().flat_map(|i|std::iter::once(i.table.clone()).chain(i.policy.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies))).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   309	    for _ in 0..bound.schema.tables.len(){let old=relevant.clone();for name in &old{for kind in bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=kind.required(){relevant.insert(table.clone());}}}if old==relevant{break;}}
   310	    let has_lifecycle=relevant.iter().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..})&&!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).contains(c)));
   311	    if !has_lifecycle{return;}
   312	    let transitions=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!bp.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&bp.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)).filter(|bp|{
   313	        ir::write_tables(&bp.program).iter().any(|(name,kind)|relevant.contains(name)&&(*kind!="insert"||bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|&i.table==name)))
   314	    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   315	    if transitions.is_empty(){return;}
   316	    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   317	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
   318	    let Ok(root)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{return;};
   319	    let mut frontier=vec![(root,Vec::<Step>::new())];
   320	    fn signature(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->String{
   321	        // Equality of this projection is only an exploration heuristic. Missing
   322	        // worlds are explicitly uncovered; it never discharges an obligation.
   323	        let projected=rows.iter().map(|(name,rs)|{let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];let mut images=rs.iter().map(|r|t.columns.iter().filter(|(c,k)|c.as_str()!="id"&&matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..}|C::Bool|C::Principal|C::Ref{..})).filter_map(|(c,_)|r.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>()).collect::<Vec<_>>();images.sort_by_key(|r|serde_json::to_string(r).unwrap());(name.clone(),images)}).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();serde_json::to_string(&projected).unwrap()
   324	    }
   325	    let mut seen=BTreeSet::from([signature(bound,&initial)]);let mut visited=0;
   326	    for _ in 0..8{
   327	        let mut next=Vec::new();
   328	        for (base,steps) in frontier{
   329	            let Ok(before)=readback(&base)else{continue;};
   330	            for bp in &transitions{
   331	                let p=&bp.program;
   332	                let targets=ir::write_tables(p);
   333	                if targets.iter().all(|(t,_)|!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()){continue;}
   334	                if targets.iter().all(|(t,kind)|*kind=="insert"&&before.get(t).is_some_and(|rs|rs.len()>=2)){continue;}
   335	                let cases=fixture_cases(bound,p,&before,&actors,out);
   336	                if cases.len()>4{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed lifecycle arguments capped at 4",p.name));}
   337	                let mut successors=0;
   338	                let program_json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
   339	                'cases:for args in cases.into_iter().take(4){
   340	                    let mut ordered=actors.iter().map(|actor|{
   341	                        let mut score=0;
   342	                        for (table,rs) in &before{let roles=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);for row in rs{
   343	                            if !bound.schema.tables[table].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&row.field(c).ok()==Some(&Value::Id(actor.clone()))){continue;}
   344	                            if !roles.is_empty()&&roles.iter().any(|c|row.field(c).is_ok_and(|v|matches!(v,Value::String(role) if program_json.contains(&format!("\"String\":\"{role}\""))))){score+=4;}
   345	                            if p.args.iter().any(|(name,k)|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Ref{table:t} if t==table)&&args.get(name)==row.field("id").ok()){score+=2;}
   346	                        }}
   347	                        if program_json.contains(&format!("\"{}\"",actor)){score+=8;}
   348	                        (actor,score)
   349	                    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();ordered.sort_by_key(|(_,score)|std::cmp::Reverse(*score));
   350	                    if ordered.len()>2{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed actor ordering keeps two candidates per argument tuple; other actors remain in breadth campaign",p.name));}
   351	                    for (viewer,_) in ordered.into_iter().take(2){
   352	                    if visited>=2048||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle successors capped at 2048 attempts/shared campaign budget; remaining histories uncovered".into());return;}visited+=1;
   353	                    let Ok(mut engine)=base.fork_fixture()else{continue;};
   354	                    let Ok(args)=materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,&args,viewer)else{continue;};
   355	                    let mut prior=None;
   356	                    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.target!="read"&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(RuleExpr::has_reference)){if !out.reserve(1){return;}prior=base.fork_fixture().ok();}
   357	                    out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
   358	                    let trace=super::executed::trace(bound,p);
   359	                    let Ok(result)=engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW).with_fixture_trace(trace.clone()))else{continue;};
   360	                    if !matches!(result.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..}){out.refused+=1;continue;}
   361	                    let Ok(after)=readback(&engine)else{continue;};
   362	                    let mut history=steps.clone();history.push(Step{operation:p.name.clone(),viewer:viewer.clone(),args:args.clone(),result:serde_json::to_value(&result.state).unwrap()});
   363	                    super::executed::record(bound,p,&trace,&result.state,&after,&initial,viewer,&history,&mut engine,out);
   364	                    changed(bound,p,&before,&after,&initial,viewer,&history,&mut prior,&mut engine,out);
   365	                    if before==after{continue;}
   366	                    out.sequences+=1;
   367	                    if !seen.insert(signature(bound,&after)){out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle signature merges worlds differing in non-status payload; remaining worlds uncovered".into());continue;}
   368	                    observe(bound,&mut engine,&after,&initial,&actors,&history,out);
   369	                    column_reads::after_history(bound,&engine,&initial,&history,out);
   370	                    let progress=after.iter().map(|(name,rs)|{let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];rs.iter().map(|r|t.columns.iter().map(|(c,k)|match k.required(){C::Enum{..} if !super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).contains(c)=>before.get(name).into_iter().flatten().find(|old|old.field("id").ok()==r.field("id").ok()).is_some_and(|old|old.field(c).ok()!=r.field(c).ok()) as usize*4,C::Bool=>matches!(r.field(c),Ok(Value::Bool(true))) as usize,_=>0}).sum::<usize>()).sum::<usize>()}).sum::<usize>();
   371	                    next.push((engine,history,progress));
   372	                    successors+=1;if successors>=2{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed successor selection capped at two new worlds per operation/world",p.name));break 'cases;}
   373	                }}
   374	            }
   375	        }
   376	        if next.is_empty(){return;}
   377	        next.sort_by_key(|(_,_,priority)|std::cmp::Reverse(*priority));
   378	        if next.len()>4{out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle beam keeps four priority worlds (at most 16 allowed); remaining histories uncovered".into());next.truncate(4);}
   379	        frontier=next.into_iter().map(|(e,h,_)|(e,h)).collect();
   380	    }
   381	    out.unsupported.insert("directed lifecycle traces bounded to eight authored mutations; longer traces uncovered".into());
   382	}
   383	pub(super) fn run(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
   384	    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
   385	    let initial=match readback(fixture){Ok(r)=>r,Err(e)=>{out.unsupported.insert(e);return;}};
   386	    if initial.values().map(Vec::len).sum::<usize>()>1000{out.unsupported.insert("history fixture exceeds 1000 rows".into());return;}
   387	    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
   388	    let probes=Vec::new();
   389	    let mut groups=Vec::new();
   390	    for bp in bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)){
   391	        let cases=fixture_cases(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,out);
   392	        let mut group=relationship_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out);
   393	        group.extend(role_candidates(bound,&bp.program,&initial,&actors,&cases,fixture,out));
   394	        // Diagonal order gives both actors and argument values an early turn.
   395	        let mut seen=group.iter().map(|(_,viewer,args)|serde_json::to_string(&(viewer,args)).unwrap()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   396	        // Give every actor the same first argument tuple before diagonal
   397	        // variation; a referee must not see only the overturned bout by chance.
   398	        if let Some(args)=cases.first(){for actor in &actors{
   399	            if group.len()>=LIMIT||out.exhausted(){break;}
   400	            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   401	        }}
   402	        'diagonal:for shift in 0..actors.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(actors.len()){
   403	            if cases.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){break 'diagonal;}
   404	            if group.len()>=LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: history candidate actors/arguments capped at 512",bp.program.name));break 'diagonal;}
   405	            let actor=&actors[(n+shift)%actors.len()];let args=&cases[n%cases.len()];
   406	            if seen.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(actor,args)).unwrap()){group.push((&bp.program,actor.clone(),args.clone()));}
   407	        }}
   408	        if !group.is_empty(){groups.push(group);}
   409	    }
   410	    let mut candidates=Vec::new();let mut candidate_keys=BTreeSet::new();
   411	    // Front-load a small fair frontier of role and relationship changes so
   412	    // ordinary no-op/rejected mutations cannot consume all observer work first.
   413	    for group in &groups {
   414	        let p=group[0].0;
   415	        let priority=ir::write_tables(p).iter().any(|(t,kind)|*kind!="insert"&&(!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,t).is_empty()||p.args.values().any(|k|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Principal))));
   416	        if priority{for c in group.iter().take(8){if candidate_keys.insert(serde_json::to_string(&(&c.0.name,&c.1,&c.2)).unwrap()){candidates.push(c.clone());if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}}}}
   417	        if candidates.len()>=LIMIT/4{break;}

   420	    if !out.reserve(1){return;}

   428	        }}
   429	    }
   430	    observe(bound,&mut base,&initial,&initial,&actors,&[],out);
   431	    // Spend half the pair budget on grant changes followed by an operation
   432	    // as the affected principal; interleave first steps and successor names.
   433	    let mut role_groups=Vec::new();
   434	    for (i,(p,_,args)) in candidates.iter().enumerate().take(LIMIT){
   435	        let json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();

     1	//! Deterministic role populations validated by the ordinary seed boundary.

     4	/// No network, credentials, development doors, durable store or scratch files.
     5	fn proposals(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
     6	    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fixture world not reserved; remaining initialization uncovered".into());}
     7	    let mut people=Vec::new();
     8	    for (table,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
     9	        let role_columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
    10	        for column in role_columns{
    11	            {
    12	                for role in super::super::privilege::role_values(bound,table,&column) {for n in 0..2 {people.push((table.clone(),column.clone(),role.clone(),format!("account:security-{table}-{role}-{n}")));}}
    13	            }
    14	        }
    15	    }
    16	    for seat in histories::viewers(bound).into_iter().filter(|p|p.starts_with("service:")&&p!="service:security") {for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-{}-{n}",seat.replace(':',"-"))));}}
    17	    if people.is_empty(){for n in 0..2{people.push((String::new(),String::new(),String::new(),format!("account:security-owner-{n}")));}}
    18	    if people.len()>64{people.truncate(64);out.unsupported.insert("generated roster capped at 64 principals; additional roles remain uncovered".into());}
    19	    let mut grant_tables=BTreeSet::new();
    20	    fn grants(rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
    21	        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|v|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{out.insert(table.clone());},
    22	        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{grants(r,out)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>grants(r,out),_=>{}
    23	    }}
    24	    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for rule in std::iter::once(&t.rules.read).chain(t.column_reads.values()){grants(rule,&mut grant_tables);}}
    25	    let counts=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(table,_)|bound.schema.maintain_for(table).is_none()).map(|(table,t)|{
    26	        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).count();(table.clone(),if own>0{own}else if grant_tables.contains(table)||std::iter::once(&t.rules.read).chain(t.column_reads.values()).any(|rule|rule.any_term(&|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if t.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal))))){people.len().max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3))}else{t.columns.values().filter_map(|k|if let C::Enum{variants}=k.required(){Some(variants.len())}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(2).max(1usize<<t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count().min(3)).clamp(2,8)})
    27	    }).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
    28	    let mut rows=BTreeMap::new();
    29	    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
    30	        if !counts.contains_key(table){continue;}
    31	        let own=people.iter().filter(|(roster,_,_,_)|roster==table).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    32	        if t.columns.values().filter(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)).count()>3 {out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: full Cartesian combinations above three principal columns remain uncovered"));}
    33	        let mut population=Vec::new();
    34	        for n in 0..counts[table]{
    35	            let person=if own.is_empty(){&people[n%people.len()]}else{own[n]};
    36	            let mut fields=BTreeMap::from([("id".into(),Value::Id(format!("security-{table}-{n}")))]);
    37	            let mut principal=0;
    38	            for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
    39	                if column=="id"||t.engine_field(column)||t.recorded.contains_key(column)||t.maintained.contains_key(column){continue;}
    40	                let mut value=if kind.is_optional()&&n%2==0&&!(person.0==*table&&person.1==*column){Value::Null}else{match sample(kind.required(),4+n){Some(value)=>value,None=>{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}.{column}: unsupported typed sample"));continue;}}};
    41	                if value==Value::Null&&kind.is_optional(){fields.insert(column.clone(),value);continue;}
    42	                match kind.required(){
    43	                    C::Principal=>{
    44	                        let linked=!own.is_empty()||grant_tables.contains(table)||t.constraints.iter().any(|constraint|matches!(constraint,Constraint::FinalExistsOnInsert{key,..} if key.iter().any(|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c) if c==column))));
    45	                        value=Value::Id(if linked{if principal==0{person.3.clone()}else{people[(n+principal)%people.len()].3.clone()}}else{if (n>>principal.min(5))&1==0{VIEWER}else{OTHER}.into()});principal+=1;
    46	                    },
    47	                    C::Ref{table:target}=>{value=Value::Id(format!("security-{target}-{}",n%counts.get(target).copied().unwrap_or(1)));},
    48	                    C::Enum{..}|C::Text{..} if person.0==*table&&person.1==*column=>value=Value::String(person.2.clone()),
    49	                    C::Text{max}=>{let minimum=t.constraints.iter().filter_map(|c|if let Constraint::Text{field,min,..}=c{(field==column).then_some(*min as usize)}else{None}).max().unwrap_or(1);let suffix=format!("sample{n}");value=Value::String(format!("{suffix}{}","x".repeat(minimum.saturating_sub(suffix.len()))).chars().take(*max as usize).collect());},
    50	                    C::Time=>value=Value::Int(NOW+if t.expire.as_deref()==Some(column){86_400_000}else{-1000}),
    51	                    _=>{},
    52	                }
    53	                fields.insert(column.clone(),value);
    54	            }
    55	            population.push(Value::Row(fields.into()));
    56	        }
    57	        rows.insert(table.clone(),population);
    58	    }
    59	    // Batch validation preserves final-exists, parent ordering and maintains.
    60	    if let Ok(engine)=engine_with(bound,&rows,Vec::new()){return Ok(engine);}
    61	    if !out.reserve(1){return Err("generated fallback world not reserved; initialization uncovered".into());}
    62	    let mut engine=engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new())?;
    63	    let mut pending=rows.into_iter().flat_map(|(table,rows)|rows.into_iter().map(move|row|(table.clone(),row))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    64	    let mut reasons=BTreeMap::new();
    65	    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
    66	        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
    67	        for (table,mut row) in pending {
    68	            if !out.reserve(1){validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
    69	            let attempt=(||{
    70	                for (column,kind) in &bound.schema.tables[&table].columns {if let Ok(value)=row.field(column){let value=hydrate(&mut engine,kind,value)?;if let Value::Row(fields)=&mut row{fields.insert(column.clone(),value);}}}
    71	                engine.seed_at([(table.clone(),row.clone())],NOW).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;Ok::<_,String>(())
    72	            })();
    73	            if let Err(why)=attempt{reasons.insert(table.clone(),why);retry.push((table,row));}
    74	        }
    75	        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
    76	    }
    77	    for (table,why) in reasons{if pending.iter().any(|(name,_)|name==&table){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: some related rows declined by ordinary validation: {why}"));}}
    78	    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;
    79	    Ok(engine)
    80	}

    82	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#137-wave-19-correction-admissible-starts-and-persistent-refutations — creator guards/copies are part of generated initialization.
    83	pub fn fixture(bound:&Bound,out:&mut Campaign)->Result<Engine,String>{
    84	    let proposed=proposals(bound,out)?;
    85	    let rows=readback(&proposed)?;
    86	    let actors=histories::actors(bound,&rows);
    87	    // A principal key and a field named role are insufficient: payload tables
    88	    // must execute their creators even when they happen to have that shape.
    89	    fn authority_roster(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool{
    90	        let columns=super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    91	        fn used(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,name:&str,columns:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{match rule{
    92	            RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|v|matches!(v,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>super::super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|columns.contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)))),
    93	            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|r|used(bound,r,name,columns)),RuleExpr::Not(r)=>used(bound,r,name,columns),_=>false,
    94	        }}
    95	        !columns.is_empty()&&bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|[&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].into_iter().chain(t.column_reads.values()).any(|r|used(bound,r,name,&columns)))
    96	    }
    97	    let rosters=bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,_)|authority_roster(bound,name)).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    98	    let mut creators=bound.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!p.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&p.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)&&ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert"))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    99	    creators.sort_by_key(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program).iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"update"|"upsert"|"delete")));
   100	    let created=creators.iter().flat_map(|p|ir::write_tables(&p.program)).filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   101	    let mut engine=engine_on_store(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new(),proposed.store().empty_with_asset_ledger().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?)?;
   102	    let mut pending=rows.iter().filter(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(*t)||!created.contains(*t)).flat_map(|(t,rs)|rs.iter().map(move|r|(t.clone(),r.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   103	    // Bootstrap proposals require an actor admitted by the insert rule in the
   104	    // previous world. Never let a proposed role authorize its own insertion.
   105	    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len(){
   106	        let before=pending.len();let mut retry=Vec::new();
   107	        for (table,row) in pending{
   108	            if !out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated initialization work exhausted; remaining populations uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
   109	            let seeded=clean_row(bound,&table,&row);
   110	            let mut accepted=None;
   111	            for actor in &actors{
   112	                if !out.reserve(1){break;}
   113	                // This only orders ordinary replay work: a definitely denied
   114	                // pre-insert gate cannot be rescued by copying the entire
   115	                // ledger. Unknown and defaults still go through the engine.
   116	                if matches!(engine.fixture_policy(&bound.schema.tables[&table].rules.insert,actor,None,&seeded,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Denied)){continue;}
   117	                let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   118	                if matches!(candidate.insert_fixture(&table,seeded.clone(),actor,NOW),Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})){accepted=Some(candidate);break;}
   119	            }
   120	            if let Some(candidate)=accepted{engine=candidate;}else{retry.push((table,row));}
   121	        }
   122	        pending=retry;if pending.len()==before{break;}
   123	    }
   124	    for (table,_) in pending{out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: no admitted insert/bootstrap; population uncovered"));}
   125	    // Try principals in the admitted authority rosters before outsider probes.
   126	    // This only orders candidates: every creator and recipient guard still
   127	    // executes normally, and outsider actors remain in the finite campaign.
   128	    // Avoid spending the short check budget hydrating media for absent roles.
   129	    let mut creator_actors=actors.clone();
   130	    let admitted=readback(&engine)?;
   131	    let roster_principals=rosters.iter().flat_map(|name|admitted.get(name).into_iter().flatten().flat_map(|row|{
   132	        bound.schema.tables[name].columns.iter().filter_map(move |(column,kind)|{
   133	            if matches!(kind.required(),C::Principal){match row.field(column){Ok(Value::Id(p))=>Some(p.clone()),_=>None}}else{None}
   134	        })
   135	    })).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
   136	    creator_actors.sort_by_key(|actor|!roster_principals.contains(actor));
   137	    // Authored insertion bodies preserve requires, immutable copies, defaults,
   138	    // role checks on principal arguments and multi-table creation invariants.
   139	    let mut attempts=0usize;let mut root_observed=false;
   140	    for _ in 0..=bound.schema.tables.len().min(12){
   141	        let before=readback(&engine)?;
   142	        for bp in &creators{
   143	            let p=&bp.program;let writes=ir::write_tables(p);
   144	            // Preserve a pre-transition root population. Later derived creators
   145	            // can otherwise consume every pending lifecycle row during setup.
   146	            if !root_observed&&writes.iter().any(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"update"|"upsert"|"delete")){
   147	                root_observed=true;
   148	                if out.generated_fixture&&out.work_remaining.is_none()&&validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine).is_ok(){
   149	                    let first=out.violations.len();histories::lifecycle_observers(bound,&engine,out);
   150	                    for witness in &mut out.violations[first..]{if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace{witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated root fixture before derived creator transitions; initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}}
   151	                }
   152	            }
   153	            let implicit=stability::implicit(bound,p);
   154	            if writes.iter().all(|(t,_)|rosters.contains(t)){continue;}
   155	            let current=readback(&engine)?;
   156	            let targets=writes.iter().filter(|(_,kind)|matches!(*kind,"insert"|"upsert")).map(|(t,_)|t).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   157	            if targets.iter().all(|t|current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){continue;}
   158	            let cases=histories::fixture_cases(bound,p,&current,&creator_actors,out);
   159	            'cases:for args in cases.into_iter().take(16){for actor in &creator_actors{
   160	                if attempts>=2048||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("generated authored initialization capped at 2048 calls; remaining creators uncovered".into());validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;return Ok(engine);}
   161	                attempts+=1;
   162	                if targets.len()==1{
   163	                    let gate=&bound.schema.tables[targets[0]].rules.insert;
   164	                    let stable=!gate.any_term(&|term|!matches!(term,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_)))
   165	                        &&kernel::dependencies(gate).iter().all(|dep|!implicit.contains(dep)&&!writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==dep));
   166	                    if stable&&matches!(engine.fixture_policy(gate,actor,None,&Value::Null,NOW),Ok(rules::Verdict::Denied)){continue;}
   167	                }
   168	                let mut candidate=engine.fork_fixture().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
   169	                let args=match materialize_arguments(&mut candidate,&p.args,&args,actor){Ok(a)=>a,Err(_)=>continue};
   170	                let result=candidate.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(actor,args).at(NOW));
   171	                if matches!(result,Ok(r) if matches!(r.state,snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent{..})) {
   172	                    let after=readback(&candidate)?;
   173	                    if targets.iter().any(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>current.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)) {engine=candidate;}
   174	                    let after=readback(&engine)?;
   175	                    if targets.iter().all(|t|after.get(*t).map_or(0,Vec::len)>=2){break 'cases;}
   176	                }
   177	            }}
   178	        }
   179	        if readback(&engine)?==before{break;}
   180	    }
   181	    for table in created.difference(&rosters){if engine.store().rows(table).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.is_empty(){out.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture {table}: authored creator did not populate this table"));}}
   182	    validate_fixture(bound,&mut engine)?;Ok(engine)
   183	}
   184	fn clean_row(bound:&Bound,table:&str,row:&Value)->Value{
   185	    let t=&bound.schema.tables[table];
   186	    match row{Value::Row(fields)=>Value::Row(fields.iter().filter(|(c,_)|!t.engine_field(c)&&!t.recorded.contains_key(*c)&&!t.maintained.contains_key(*c)).map(|(c,v)|(c.clone(),v.clone())).collect()),_=>row.clone()}
   187	}

//! Role authority is compared by effective gates, never by names like 'admin'.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — advisory grant-edge analysis, not a security proof or implicit exemption.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn probe_pairs(bound:&Bound,table:&str,index:&str,key:&[RuleTerm],equalities:&[(String,RuleTerm)])->Vec<(String,RuleTerm)>{
    let mut pairs=equalities.to_vec();
    if let Some(idx)=bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.index(index)){for (component,term) in idx.components.iter().zip(key){if let Some(column)=component.column(){pairs.push((column.into(),term.clone()));}}}
    pairs
}
pub(super) fn global_principal(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->Option<String>{
    let t=bound.schema.table(table)?;
    t.indexes.values().find_map(|index|{
        if !index.unique||index.components.len()!=1{return None;}
        let column=index.components[0].column()?;
        matches!(t.columns.get(column),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)).then(||column.to_string())
    })
}
pub(super) fn membership_probe(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{
    let RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=rule else{return false;};
    if table!=roster{return false;}
    let Some(principal)=global_principal(bound,table)else{return false;};
    let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
    pairs.len()==1&&pairs[0].0==principal&&pairs[0].1==RuleTerm::Viewer
}
// @ref LLP 3000.015#1 — ownership is required for every supplied owned field;
// only an exclusively owned patch bypasses an unnamed row update gate.
fn write_admission(table:&snapback4_core::schema::Table,op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->RuleExpr{
    let touched=fields.iter().filter(|f|!f.spread&&f.name!="id").map(|f|f.name.as_str()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    let owned_only=!touched.is_empty()&&!fields.iter().any(|f|f.spread)&&touched.iter().all(|f|table.written_by.contains_key(*f));
    let gate=match op{
        ast::WriteOp::Insert=>table.rules.insert.clone(),
        ast::WriteOp::Update if owned_only&&!table.update_transition=>RuleExpr::Allow,
        ast::WriteOp::Update=>table.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),
        _=>RuleExpr::Deny,
    };
    let mut owners=table.written_by.iter().filter(|(field,authority)|touched.contains(field.as_str())||op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&!authority.audience&&table.rules.update.needs_field_value(field,&table.columns[*field])).map(|(_,authority)|RuleExpr::Eq(if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{RuleTerm::Field(authority.owner.clone())}else{RuleTerm::OldField(authority.owner.clone())},RuleTerm::Viewer)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    owners.push(RuleExpr::Or(gate.disjuncts().iter().filter(|r|r.service_grant().is_none()).map(|r|(*r).clone()).collect()));
    RuleExpr::And(owners)
}
pub(super) fn membership_grant_risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,operation:&str,requirements:&[RuleExpr],op:ast::WriteOp,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->bool{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Insert,fields)||membership_grant_risk(bound,roster,operation,requirements,ast::WriteOp::Update,fields);}
    fn honors(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str)->bool{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster)),
        r=>membership_probe(bound,r,roster),
    }}
    if global_principal(bound,roster).is_none()||!bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|super::columns::rules(t).any(|r|honors(bound,r,roster))){return false;}
    let table=&bound.schema.tables[roster];
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&global_principal(bound,roster).is_some_and(|column|kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.clone()),RuleTerm::Field(column)))==Ok(true)){return false;}
    let ordinary=substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster","$membership",None,None);
    bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.is_none()&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).any(|p|super::operation::safe(p)&&kernel::implies(&ordinary,p)==Ok(false))
}
fn substitute(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,role:Option<&str>,target:Option<&str>)->RuleExpr{
    let known=|t:&RuleTerm|match t{RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c) if c==column=>target.map(|s|snapback4_core::Value::String(s.into())),RuleTerm::Literal(v)=>Some(v.clone()),_=>None};
    match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,index,equalities,..} if table==roster&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
            let pairs=probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities);
            let requested=pairs.iter().find(|(c,_)|c==column).and_then(|(_,t)|if let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{Some(s.as_str())}else{None});
            if requested.is_none(){rule.clone()}else if requested==role{
                if pairs.iter().all(|(c,t)|c==column||matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{rule.clone()}
            }else{RuleExpr::Deny}
        },
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::Id(seat)),RuleTerm::Viewer) if seat.starts_with("service:")=>RuleExpr::Deny,
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>if let (Some(a),Some(b))=(known(a),known(b)){if (a==b)==matches!(rule,RuleExpr::Eq(..)){RuleExpr::Allow}else{RuleExpr::Deny}}else{rule.clone()},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>RuleExpr::Or(xs.iter().map(|r|substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target)).collect()),
        RuleExpr::Not(r)=>RuleExpr::Not(Box::new(substitute(bound,r,roster,column,role,target))),
        _=>rule.clone(),
    }
}
pub(super) fn risk(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str,operation:&str,targets:&[String],op:ast::WriteOp,requirements:&[RuleExpr],dynamic:bool,fields:&[ast::ObjectField])->Option<String>{
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Upsert{return risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Insert,requirements,dynamic,fields).or_else(||risk(bound,roster,column,operation,targets,ast::WriteOp::Update,requirements,dynamic,fields));}
    let table=bound.schema.table(roster)?;
    if requirements.iter().any(|r|!r.disjuncts().is_empty()&&r.disjuncts().iter().all(|b|b.service_grant().is_some())){return None;}
    let roles=role_values(bound,roster,column);
    let intents=bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==roster&&i.column.as_deref().is_none_or(|c|c==column)&&(i.operation.as_deref()==Some(operation)||i.operation.is_none()&&(i.target=="write"||i.target==if op==ast::WriteOp::Insert{"insert"}else{"update"}))).filter_map(|i|i.policy.as_ref()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    fn role_guards(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut Vec<RuleExpr>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))&&probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,v)|role_columns(bound,table).contains(c)&&matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))))=>out.push(rule.clone()),
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{role_guards(bound,r,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(std::iter::once(write_admission(table,op,fields)).chain(requirements.iter().cloned()).collect());
    if op==ast::WriteOp::Update&&kernel::implies(&admission,&RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.into()),RuleTerm::Field(column.into())))==Ok(true){return None;}
    let mut guards=Vec::new();role_guards(bound,&admission,&mut guards);
    let independent=kernel::implies(&substitute(bound,&admission,"$no-roster",column,None,None),&RuleExpr::Or(guards))==Ok(true);
    let mut ranked=roles.iter().filter(|r|targets.is_empty()||targets.contains(r)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let score=|role:&&String|bound.schema.visible_tables().flat_map(|(_,t)|super::columns::effects(t)).filter(|(_,rule)|kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&substitute(bound,rule,roster,column,Some(role.as_str()),None))==Ok(true)).count();
    ranked.sort_by_key(|role|(std::cmp::Reverse(score(role)),role.as_str()));
    for target in ranked{
        for caller in std::iter::once(None).chain(roles.iter().map(|s|Some(s.as_str()))){
            if caller==Some(target.as_str()){continue;}
            let admitted=substitute(bound,&admission,roster,column,caller,Some(target));
            let admits=kernel::implies(&admitted,&RuleExpr::Deny)!=Ok(true);
            if !admits{continue;}
            let violates_intent=intents.iter().any(|p|kernel::implies(&admitted,&substitute(bound,p,roster,column,caller,Some(target)))==Ok(false));
            // Existing independently gated appointers may delegate different
            // roles. Independent intent can restrict a particular target role.
            if independent&&!violates_intent{continue;}
            if violates_intent{return Some(format!("{roster}.{column} assignment to '{target}' is not established by the independent delegation intent for {}",caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (effect,rule) in super::columns::effects(t){
                let granted=substitute(bound,&rule,roster,column,Some(target),None);let previous=substitute(bound,&rule,roster,column,caller,None);
                if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&granted)!=Ok(true){continue;}
                if !dynamic&&!((matches!(effect.as_str(),"read"|"delete")||effect.ends_with(" read"))||!role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()){continue;}
                if kernel::implies(&granted,&previous)==Ok(false){return Some(format!("{} can confer '{target}', which adds {name}.{effect} authority not held by {}",roster,caller.unwrap_or("a caller without a role")));}
            }}
        }
    }None
}

pub(super) fn role_columns(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut columns=BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(table){for c in ["role","kind"]{if t.columns.contains_key(c){columns.insert(c.into());}}}
    fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,table:&str,columns:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table:t,index,key,equalities,..} if t==table&&key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|k|matches!(k,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>for (c,v) in &probe_pairs(bound,t,index,key,equalities){if matches!(v,RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(_))){columns.insert(c.clone());}},
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,table,columns)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,table,columns),_=>{}
    }}
    let named=columns.clone();let mut observed=BTreeSet::new();
    for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in super::columns::rules(t){collect(bound,r,table,&mut observed);}}
    let global=bound.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t|t.indexes.values().any(|index|{
        if !index.unique{return false;}
        let Some(parts)=index.components.iter().map(|p|p.column()).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>()else{return false;};
        let principals=parts.iter().filter(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(**c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))).count();
        if principals!=1{return false;}
        parts.iter().all(|c|matches!(t.columns.get(*c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal))||named.contains(*c))
            ||named.is_empty()&&observed.len()==1&&parts.len()==2&&parts.iter().all(|c|observed.contains(*c)||matches!(t.columns.get(*c).map(|k|k.required()),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)))
    }));
    if global{for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in super::columns::rules(t){collect(bound,r,table,&mut columns);}}}else{columns.clear();}
    columns
}

pub(super) fn role_values(bound:&Bound,roster:&str,column:&str)->Vec<String>{
    let Some(table)=bound.schema.table(roster)else{return Vec::new();};
    let Some(kind)=table.columns.get(column)else{return Vec::new();};
    match kind.required(){snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Enum{variants}=>variants.clone(),snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Text{..}=>{
        let mut roles=BTreeSet::new();
        fn collect(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,roster:&str,column:&str,roles:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
            RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if table==roster=>for (c,t) in &probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities){if c==column&&let RuleTerm::Literal(snapback4_core::Value::String(s))=t{roles.insert(s.clone());}},
            RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>collect(bound,r,roster,column,roles),_=>{}
        }}
        for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for r in super::columns::rules(t){collect(bound,r,roster,column,&mut roles);}}roles.into_iter().collect()
    },_=>Vec::new()}
}
//! Advice identifies a risk shape; it never supplies a proof assumption.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#123-actionable-authority-advice — current roles, grant writers, required evidence and refusal oracles.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Expr,Stmt,ProgramKind};
const NOW_FOR_DEFAULTS:i64=1_800_000_000_000;
fn rule_parts(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<&RuleExpr>{match rule{RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().flat_map(rule_parts).collect(),RuleExpr::Not(x)=>rule_parts(x),r=>vec![r]}}
fn role_test(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {rule_parts(rule).iter().any(|r|match r{
    RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..} if key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,v)|v)).any(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer))=>{
        if super::privilege::membership_probe(bound,r,table){return true;}
        let columns=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
        super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,t)|columns.contains(c)&&matches!(t,RuleTerm::Literal(_)))
    },_=>false,
})}
fn reader_columns(rule:&RuleExpr)->BTreeSet<String>{rule_parts(rule).into_iter().filter_map(|r|match r{RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))=>Some(f.clone()),_=>None}).collect()}
fn note(notes:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>,site:&Site,code:&str,message:String,rewrite:&str){notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,message,rewrite).guide("security"));}
pub(super) fn mutable_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str)->bool {
    let Some(t)=bound.schema.table(name)else{return false;};
    let mut roles=super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name);
    if roles.is_empty()&&let Some(principal)=super::privilege::global_principal(bound,name){roles.insert(principal);}
    if roles.is_empty(){return false;}
    let mutable=roles.iter().any(|c|!t.constraints.iter().any(|k|matches!(k,snapback4_core::schema::Constraint::Immutable{fields} if fields.contains(c))));
    fn supplied(value:&serde_json::Value,roles:&BTreeSet<String>)->bool{
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Object").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        if let Some(fields)=value.get("Literal").and_then(|v|v.get("Map").or_else(||v.get("Row"))).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object){return fields.keys().any(|c|roles.contains(c));}
        true // Dynamic patches cannot establish that role fields are untouched.
    }
    fn writes(value:&serde_json::Value,name:&str,roles:&BTreeSet<String>,update:bool,delete:bool)->bool{
        match value{
            serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>{
                for tag in ["Update","Upsert","WriteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="WriteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if update&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name)&&body.get(if tag=="Upsert"{"row"}else{"patch"}).is_some_and(|v|supplied(v,roles)){return true;}
                }}
                for tag in ["Delete","DeleteTarget"]{if let Some(body)=xs.get(tag){
                    let target=if tag=="DeleteTarget"{body.get("target").and_then(|v|v.get("table"))}else{body.get("table")};
                    if delete&&target.and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str)==Some(name){return true;}
                }}
                xs.values().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete))
            },serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(|v|writes(v,name,roles,update,delete)),_=>false,
        }
    }
    bound.programs.iter().any(|p|{
        let system=p.program.kind==snapback4_core::ir::ProgramKind::Job;
        let update=mutable&&(system||!matches!(t.rules.update,RuleExpr::Deny)||roles.iter().any(|c|t.written_by.contains_key(c)));
        let delete=system||!matches!(t.rules.delete,RuleExpr::Deny);
        writes(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),name,&roles,update,delete)
    })
}
fn recipient_role(bound:&Bound,name:&str,branch:&RuleExpr,grant:Option<&str>,roster:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    if bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&(i.target==effect||i.target=="write"&&matches!(effect,"insert"|"update"))&&i.policy.as_ref().is_some_and(|p|kernel::dependencies(p).contains(roster)&&role_test(bound,p)&&kernel::implies(branch,p)!=Ok(true))){return true;}
    // This is conditional lifetime advice, not an inferred current-role
    // contract: the retained grant does not itself consult this mutable roster.
    grant.is_some()&&!kernel::dependencies(branch).contains(roster)
}
pub(super) fn notes(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let mut notes=Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#131-intent-and-diagnostics — equivalence is advice, never refusal.
    for inv in &bound.invariants {
        if inv.operation.is_some()||inv.target=="always" {continue;}
        let Some(policy)=&inv.policy else{continue;};
        let table=&bound.schema.tables[&inv.table];
        let mut admissions=Vec::new();
        let restates=super::effects(inv).iter().all(|word|{
            if matches!(*word,"insert"|"update")&&!table.written_by.is_empty(){return false;}
            let gate=if *word=="read"{super::columns::admission(table,inv.column.as_deref())}else if *word=="update"{super::images::stable(table,&table.update_rule_judged())}else{table.rules.for_effect(super::effect(word)).clone()};
            let intent=if *word=="update"{super::images::stable(table,&policy.old_and_next())}else{policy.clone()};
            let matches=super::intent::restates(bound,&inv.table,word,&gate,&intent);
            admissions.push((word.to_string(),gate));
            matches
        });
        if restates {
            let target=inv.column.as_ref().map(|c|format!("{}.{c}",inv.table)).unwrap_or(inv.table.clone());
            let questions=admissions.iter().map(|(word,gate)|super::intent::questions(bound,&target,word,gate)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            note(&mut notes,&inv.site,"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE",format!("{} matches or weakens current enforcement. {}",inv.target_label(),questions.join(" ")),"answer each audience question from the app specification; keep confirmed intent, and narrow unintended access in the rule. Review the per-role/per-column questionnaire in snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft; run check -> test -> audit");}
    }

    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        notes.extend(super::advice::read_notes(bound,name,t));
        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
            use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType as C;
            if kind.contains_asset() && (kind.is_optional() || matches!(kind.required(),C::List{..}|C::ListRange{min:0,..})) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE",format!("{name}.{column} permits omitted or empty media evidence"),"if the spec requires evidence, declare a required image/file or [image] 1..N; otherwise retain optionality deliberately (guide security)");
            }
        }
        for leaf in rule_parts(&t.rules.insert) {
            if let RuleExpr::Created {table:parent,..}=leaf
                && bound.schema.table(parent).is_some_and(|p| p.rules.insert.disjuncts().iter().any(|r| matches!(r,RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(_),RuleTerm::Viewer)|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(_)))))
                && bound.schema.tables.values().any(|other| super::columns::rules(other).any(|r| rule_parts(r).iter().any(|r|matches!(r,RuleExpr::Exists{table,key,equalities,..} if table==name&&matches!(key.as_slice(),[RuleTerm::Viewer])&&equalities.is_empty())))) {
                note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_VACUOUS_RULE",format!("{name}: `created {parent}` can bootstrap authority trusted globally by a viewer-only probe; a caller may create their own global administrator grant"),"provision global authority only through an operator/service seat or existing authorized admin; group creation must scope every later grant to that group (guide deploy, first-admin setup)");
            }
        }
        for (label,rule) in super::columns::effects(t) {
          let (effect,column)=label.strip_suffix(" read").map(|column|("read",Some(column))).unwrap_or((label.as_str(),None));
          for branch in rule.disjuncts(){
            for dep in kernel::dependencies(branch){if let Some(g)=bound.schema.table(&dep) {
                let rosters=[&g.rules.insert,&g.rules.update].into_iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).filter(|r|r!=&dep&&mutable_role(bound,r)).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
                if rosters.iter().any(|r|recipient_role(bound,name,branch,Some(&dep),r,effect))&&(role_test(bound,&g.rules.insert)||role_test(bound,&g.rules.update)){
                    note(&mut notes,&Site::default(),"N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE",format!("{name}.{label}: `{}` trusts retained {dep} grants that can survive role changes in {}; confirm whether current-role intent is required",branch.quarry(),rosters.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),"recheck the current role in this branch or revoke every derived grant transactionally on role changes; guide security");
                }
            }}
-- Wash Fold Laundry — Snapback 4 (Quarry) schema, v1.
-- Roles: customer, attendant, driver, owner (roster in `roles`).
-- Operators bootstrap via the service:operator seat; owners manage staff after that.

use identity password

table roles:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('customer', 'attendant', 'driver', 'owner')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  online only

table driverRoutes:
  driver: principal
  route: text 1..40
  unique driver
  immutable driver
  read <- .driver = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  online only

-- One bag drop-off. priceCents is computed server-side from weight (25c/oz).
table orders:
  customer: principal
  weightOz: int 1..3200
  priceCents: int 0..1000000
  tagPhoto: image <=5000000
  status: enum('washing', 'drying', 'folding', 'ready', 'pickedup', 'donated') default 'washing'
  route: text 1..40
  droppedAt: time default now
  readyAt: time ?
  pickedUpAt: time ?
  immutable customer, weightOz, droppedAt
  read <- .customer = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] or exists driverRoutes[viewer, route = .route]
  insert <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  update <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] or .customer = viewer
  online only

-- A customer's lost/damaged claim; only the owner decides it.
table claims:
  order: orders
  customer: principal
  reason: enum('lost', 'damaged')
  detail: text 1..500
  status: enum('pending', 'approved', 'denied') default 'pending'
  refundCents: int ?
  at: time default now
  unique order else ALREADY_CLAIMED 'This order already has a claim.'
  immutable order, customer, reason, detail, at
  read <- .customer = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  insert <- .customer = viewer
  update <- exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']
  online only

query myRole():
  return roles[viewer]

query allRoles(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER
  return roles first 50 by .id after c

query myOrders(c: cursor ?):
  return orders where .customer = viewer last 50 by .droppedAt after c { *, overdue: .droppedAt + 2d < now and (.status = 'washing' or .status = 'drying' or .status = 'folding') }

query receipt(order: orders):
  return orders[order]

query receipts(c: cursor ?):
  return orders where .customer = viewer and (.status = 'ready' or .status = 'pickedup') last 50 by .droppedAt after c

query board(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF
  return orders where .status = 'washing' or .status = 'drying' or .status = 'folding' or .status = 'ready' last 100 by .droppedAt after c { *, overdue: .droppedAt + 2d < now and (.status = 'washing' or .status = 'drying' or .status = 'folding') }

query flagged(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF
  return orders where .droppedAt + 2d < now and (.status = 'washing' or .status = 'drying' or .status = 'folding') last 100 by .droppedAt after c

query myRouteOrders(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'driver'] else NOT_DRIVER
  r = driverRoutes[viewer] ! else NO_ROUTE 'No route assigned to this driver.'
  return orders where .route = r.route last 100 by .droppedAt after c

query myRoute():
  return driverRoutes[viewer]

query routes(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF
  return driverRoutes last 50 by .id after c

query myClaims(c: cursor ?):
  return claims where .customer = viewer last 50 by .at after c

query allClaims(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER
  return claims last 50 by .at after c

mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('customer', 'attendant', 'driver', 'owner')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roles[principal] { principal, role }

mutation assignRoute(driver: principal, route: text 1..40):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER
  require exists roles[driver, role = 'driver'] else NOT_DRIVER
  return upsert driverRoutes[driver] { driver, route }

mutation dropOff(customer: principal, weightOz: int 1..3200, tagPhoto: image <=5000000, route: text 1..40):
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_ATTENDANT
  require exists roles[customer, role = 'customer'] else NOT_CUSTOMER 'Drop-offs are logged for customer accounts.'
  return insert orders { customer, weightOz, priceCents: weightOz * 25, tagPhoto, route }

mutation advanceStatus(order: orders, status: enum('drying', 'folding', 'ready')):
  o = orders[order] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF
  require (o.status = 'washing' and status = 'drying') or (o.status = 'drying' and status = 'folding') or (o.status = 'folding' and status = 'ready') else BAD_TRANSITION 'Bags move washing -> drying -> folding -> ready.'
  update orders[order] { status, readyAt: now if status = 'ready' else o.readyAt }
  return null

mutation pickup(order: orders):
  o = orders[order] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require o.customer = viewer or exists roles[viewer, role = 'attendant'] or exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_YOURS
  require o.status = 'ready' else NOT_READY 'This bag is not ready for pickup.'
  require (o.readyAt ?? 0) > now - 30d else TOO_LATE 'Unclaimed bags are donated after 30 days.'
  update orders[order] { status: 'pickedup', pickedUpAt: now }
  return null

mutation fileClaim(order: orders, reason: enum('lost', 'damaged'), detail: text 1..500):
  o = orders[order] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require o.customer = viewer else NOT_YOURS 'Claims are filed by the order owner.'
  return insert claims { order, customer: viewer, reason, detail }

mutation decideClaim(claim: claims, approve: bool, refundCents: int ?):
  c = claims[claim] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER 'Only the store owner approves refunds.'
  require c.status = 'pending' else ALREADY_DECIDED 'This claim was already decided.'
  require not approve or (refundCents ?? 0) > 0 else BAD_REFUND 'An approved refund needs an amount.'
  require approve or refundCents = null else BAD_REFUND 'A denied claim carries no amount.'
  update claims[claim] { status: 'approved' if approve else 'denied', refundCents }
  return null

job donateUnclaimed(periodStart: time, periodEnd: time):
  for o in orders by .id:
    if o.status = 'ready' and (o.readyAt ?? 0) < periodEnd - 30d:
      update orders[o.id] { status: 'donated' }

schedule daily every 1d at 06:00: donateUnclaimed
