# Rules

**Snapback 2 optimizes the edit-to-verified loop — the agent's building an app on it, and ours building it.**

Speed is the constraint. Everything below exists to keep the edit-to-verified loop short
and to stop this repository accreting apparatus faster than anyone can remove it. These
rules bind `snapback2/` and the LLPs numbered 1000 and up; Snapback 1 is baselined, not
exempt, and its numbers may only fall.

Each rule is **[check]** (enforced by `caps`) or **[review]** (enforced by a human).

## Budgets — every budget is a trade, not a limit

- **5 blocking checks, 60s total.** A sixth requires deleting one, in the same PR. **[check]**
- **59 documents in the working set** (`llp/current/`). Today's count. A 60th requires
  archiving one — which is just removing the link. Snapback 2 aims to leave this at 15;
  Snapback 1's links are archived at promotion. **[check]**
- **12 documents in the foundation** (`llp/foundation/`). **[check]**
- **This file: 700 words.** If it grows, something becomes a check or stops being a rule. **[check]**
- **1,500 lines per source file, baseline 204.** Generated files exempt — and generated
  files are built, not committed; so are benchmark run records under `benchmarks/runs/`,
  which are evidence, not source. The baseline is Snapback 1's oversize count on the day
  the rule landed; it may not grow, and `snapback2/` contributes zero to it. **[check]**

## Blocking checks

`caps` · `ref-check` · `snapback2/scripts/test.sh` · `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs --check` · `check-inventories`

## Loop shape

- **Every check reports all failures in one run.** Fail-fast is banned. **[check]**
- **Nothing blocks on anything slower than 60 seconds.** Slow verification runs on a
  schedule, attributed to the breaking commit, and files a bounded set of issues. **[review]**
- **Fix loops get 3 rounds.** Then stop: escalate to a human or descope. **[review]**

## Time budgets

Tracked every run of `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs` (appends `snapback2/metrics/loop.jsonl`).
A regression is a P0 with a name on it.

| | budget |
|---|---|
| Incremental rebuild after a one-line edit | 15s |
| Test what you just changed | 30s |
| Blocking gate suite | 60s |
| Full build, warm cache | 5 min |
| **`snapback2 check` on a program (the agent's product latency)** | **1s** |
| **`snapback2 doctor` boot-to-ready** | **0.5s** |

## Scope

- **`NOT-DOING.md` is binding.** Moving something onto the doing-list means writing why
  and taking something off. **[review]**
- **Delete; don't deprecate.** No compat shims or migration paths before promotion. **[review]**
- **Nothing is compiled, transpiled, or generated at runtime that could be built ahead of
  time.** **[review]**
- **A spec needs an implementer and a date, or it isn't written.** **[review]**
- **Only implemented specs bind.** A document with no running code behind it is a
  proposal, and a proposal cannot block a PR or justify a check. **[review]**

## Agents

- **Agents remove apparatus freely and add none.** An agent PR that adds a check, script,
  registry, config file, or design doc needs explicit human approval saying so. **[review]**
- **2026-09-06 session exception:** the user delegated the search implementation
  decisions; one `snapback2-core/build.rs` may generate pinned Unicode tables at
  build time, alongside the existing package build. No new gate, registry,
  runtime generator, or Unicode dependency is authorized by this exception. **[review]**
- **Snapback 2 LLPs are numbered 1000 and up**; `llp-create` must be told. **[review]**
- **Snapback 4 LLPs are numbered 3000 and up** (`snapback4/`, LLP 3000); it adopts these budgets
  and loop shape as its own and keeps its scope in `snapback4/NOT-DOING.md`. **[review]**

## Shared machines

- Warm, named lanes, one per stream of work. Never clone fresh; reuse the worktree.
- **Never `git stash`.** Use a temporary commit.
- Kill only PIDs you recorded at launch. Never by name or pattern.

//! CLI security reporting; compile/test evidence share one analyzer.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#5-exposure-report — full audit is opt-in, intent notes are on ordinary check.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::security::{self,Report};
mod http;
mod record;
pub(super) use record::{remember,capture};
pub(super) fn unauthenticated(bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)])->bool {
    match http::run(bound,sources){Ok(_)=>true,Err(why)=>{eprintln!("security HTTP failure: {why}");false}}
}
pub(super) fn report(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],options:&Options)->Report{
    let mut selection=options.clone();
    let selected_fixture=record::selected_fixture(project,options);
    if selected_fixture.is_dir(){selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),selected_fixture.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let inputs=match selection.flags.get("fixture"){
        Some(path)=>match super::test_fixture::snapshot(std::path::Path::new(path)){Ok(snapshot)=>Some(snapshot),Err(why)=>{let mut report=security::analyze_rules(bound);security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);report.campaign_coverage.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));return report;}},None=>None,
    };
    if let Some(snapshot)=&inputs{selection.flags.insert("fixture".into(),snapshot.0.to_string_lossy().into_owned());}
    let cached=record::matching(project,&selection,sources);
    let known_failure=cached.as_ref().is_some_and(|r|!r.violations.is_empty())&&!options.flags.contains_key("security-full");
    let mut campaign=if options.flags.contains_key("security-full"){security::challenges::Campaign::default()}else{security::challenges::Campaign::bounded(1500)};
    if known_failure {campaign.work_remaining=Some(0);campaign.unsupported.insert("matching full-test refutations retained; bounded resampling skipped; repair and rerun snapback4 test".into());}
    // An explicitly selected fixture remains the initial-state assumption.
    // Static generation runs only on the fixture-free path; both share this
    // one bounded campaign with all subsequent concrete exploration.
    let mut report=if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture")||known_failure{
        security::analyze_rules(bound)
    }else{security::analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut campaign)};
    security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);
    if !bound.invariants.is_empty()&&!known_failure{
        if selection.flags.contains_key("fixture"){
            match super::test_fixture::engine_at(project,bound,&selection,1_800_000_000_000){
                Ok(fixture)=>if bound.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|fixture.store().rows(t).is_ok_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())){security::challenges::fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,&mut campaign)}else{
                    // An empty starter directory selects generation, just as
                    // no fixture does. Give the direct contradiction its turn
                    // before empty-start mutation permutations spend the cap.
                    report=security::analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut campaign);
                    security::add_source_notes(&mut report,sources,bound);
                    security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false)
                },
                Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("fixture reachability not covered: {why}"));}
            }
        }else{security::challenges::generated_campaign(bound,&mut campaign,false);}
    }
    let sites=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&!campaign.violations.iter().any(|c|c.invariant_site==v.invariant.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable())).map(|v|v.invariant.site.to_string()).collect();
    let campaign=security::challenges::run_selected_after(bound,Some(&sites),campaign);
    security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&campaign);
    if let Some(record)=cached {security::challenges::apply(&mut report,&security::challenges::Campaign{violations:record.violations,..Default::default()});}
    report
}
pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let sources=match project.sources(){Ok(s)=>s,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}};
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(snapback4_lang::compile(&sources),options.json);
    // Audit is a permission map plus bounded evidence; `test` owns full campaigns.
    let report=report(&project,&bound,&sources,options);
    let dispositions=match super::security_reviews::review(&project,&sources,&report.notes,None){Ok(d)=>d,Err(error)=>{eprintln!("audit: E_SECURITY_NOTES: {error}");return 2;}};
    if options.json {let mut value=serde_json::to_value(&report).unwrap();value["securityReviews"]=serde_json::to_value(&dispositions).unwrap();println!("{value}");}
    else {print!("{}",report.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));super::security_reviews::print(&dispositions);}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}
pub(super) fn exposure(report:&Report){
    let broad=report.exposures.iter().filter(|e|e.effects.get("read").is_some_and(|r|r=="allow"||r.starts_with("public"))).count();
    eprintln!("security exposure: {} tables, {} conditional audiences, {broad} broadly readable tables; run snapback4 audit for exact role/column predicates",report.exposures.len(),report.roles.len());
}
pub(super) fn summary(report:&Report,verbose:bool){
    let proved=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Proved).count();
    let failures=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let refuted=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted).count();
    eprintln!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted, {} unproved ({} failing)",report.invariants.len()-proved-refuted,failures.len());
    for v in &failures {
        let c=v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
        eprintln!("  FIX {}: {} via {} as {} ({}); tighten enforcement to the declared intent, then run snapback4 test",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.viewer,serde_json::to_value(&c.reachability).unwrap().as_str().unwrap());
    }
    let supplemental=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|!c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    if supplemental>0 {eprintln!("  {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing)");}
    if verbose {eprint!("{}",report.text_with_all(true));}
    else if !report.invariants.is_empty()||!report.campaign_coverage.is_empty(){eprintln!("  next: snapback4 test; {} coverage gaps (check --verbose/JSON keeps every reason)",report.campaign_coverage.len());}
    if !report.exposures.is_empty(){eprintln!("  review audiences and {} uncovered exposures: snapback4 audit",report.uncovered.len());}
}

/// A rule-changing preflight demands a current campaign, without pretending
/// finite tests or undeclared intent certify a deployment.
pub(super) fn release_loop(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],prior:Option<&Bound>,options:&Options)->Result<Option<String>,String>{
    let changed=prior.is_none_or(|old|bound.schema.tables.iter().any(|(name,t)|old.schema.tables.get(name).is_none_or(|o|serde_json::to_value((&o.rules,&o.column_reads,&o.written_by)).ok()!=serde_json::to_value((&t.rules,&t.column_reads,&t.written_by)).ok()))||old.schema.tables.keys().any(|t|!bound.schema.tables.contains_key(t)));
#[serde(rename_all="lowercase")]
pub enum Status { Proved, Refuted, Unproved }
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Verdict {
    pub invariant: Invariant,
    pub status: Status,
    pub direct_rule: Status,
    pub paths: Vec<String>,
    pub reasons: Vec<String>,
    pub counterexample: Option<challenges::Counterexample>,
    pub obligations: Vec<Obligation>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Report {
    pub format: u32,
    pub scope: String,
    pub exclusions: Vec<String>,
    pub invariants: Vec<Verdict>,
    pub exposures: Vec<Exposure>,
    pub roles: BTreeMap<String,Vec<String>>,
    pub notes: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    pub uncovered: Vec<UncoveredExposure>,
    pub campaign_coverage: BTreeSet<String>,
}
impl Report {
    pub fn refuted(&self)->bool {self.invariants.iter().any(|v|v.status==Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable()))}
    pub fn text(&self)->String {self.text_with_all(false)}
    pub fn text_with_all(&self,all:bool)->String {
        let mut out=self.action_text(if all {usize::MAX} else {5});
        if all {out.push_str(&format!("security scope: {}\n",self.scope));}
        for v in &self.invariants {
            if !all && v.status != Status::Refuted {continue;}
            if !all&&v.status==Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|!c.reachability.is_reachable()){out.push_str(&format!("  {}: seed/synthetic refutation only (non-failing; --all for details)\n",v.invariant.target_label()));continue;}
            out.push_str(&format!("  {:?}: {}.{} {} — direct rule {:?}\n",v.status,v.invariant.table,format!("{}{}",v.invariant.column.as_ref().map(|c|format!("{c} ")).unwrap_or_default(),v.invariant.target),v.invariant.predicate,v.direct_rule));
            for reason in &v.reasons {out.push_str(&format!("    {reason}\n"));}
            for o in &v.obligations {out.push_str(&format!("    {:?}: {} — {}\n",o.status,o.path,o.reason));if let Some(repair)=&o.repair{out.push_str(&format!("      repair: {repair}\n"));}}
            if let Some(c)=&v.counterexample {out.push_str(&format!("    counterexample {}\n",serde_json::to_string(c).unwrap()));}
        }

        for (role,entries) in &self.roles {
            out.push_str(&format!("role {role} (possible/conditional; row predicates below decide):\n"));
            for entry in entries {out.push_str(&format!("  {entry}\n"));}
        }
        for x in &self.exposures {
            out.push_str(&format!("table {} columns: {}\n",x.table,x.columns.iter().map(|(c,v)|format!("{c} ({v})")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")));
            for (column,audience) in &x.column_audiences {out.push_str(&format!("  column {column} audience: {audience}\n"));}
            for path in &x.paths {out.push_str(&format!("  {path}\n"));}
            for grant in &x.grants {out.push_str(&format!("  inherited grant {}: {}\n",grant.table,grant.predicate)); for w in &grant.writers {out.push_str(&format!("    writer {w}\n"));}}
        }
        for reason in self.campaign_coverage.iter().filter(|_|all){out.push_str(&format!("  campaign not covered: {reason}\n"));}
        if all {out.push_str(&format!("  exclusions: {}\n",self.exclusions.join("; ")));}
        out.push_str("exposures without an invariant (conditional role/row coverage; declarations are not proof):\n");
        for x in &self.uncovered {out.push_str(&format!("  {}.{}: {}; rows: {}\n",x.table,x.effect,x.audience,x.row_predicate));}
        if self.uncovered.is_empty(){out.push_str("  none\n");}out
    }
}
fn effect(word:&str)->Effect {match word {"read"=>Effect::Read,"insert"=>Effect::Insert,"update"=>Effect::Update,_=>Effect::Delete}}
fn effects(inv:&Invariant)->Vec<&str> { if inv.operation.is_some(){vec!["insert","update","delete"]}else if inv.target=="write" {vec!["insert","update"]} else {vec![inv.target.as_str()]} }
/// Analyze declarations without using fixtures as proof assumptions.
pub fn analyze(bound:&Bound)->Report {
    analyze_with_campaign(bound,&mut challenges::Campaign::bounded(1500))
}
/// The CLI supplies its single campaign budget, shared with remaining paths.
pub fn analyze_with_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut challenges::Campaign)->Report {
    let mut report=analyze_rules(bound);
    if report.invariants.iter().any(|v|v.invariant.target=="read"&&v.direct_rule!=Status::Proved){
        challenges::direct_refutations(bound,campaign);
        challenges::apply(&mut report,campaign);
    }
    report
}
/// The independent proof pass never uses generated data as an assumption.
pub fn analyze_rules(bound:&Bound)->Report {
    let exposures=audit::exposures(bound);
    let mut verdicts=Vec::new();
    for inv in &bound.invariants {

/// Fixture-free campaigns retain empty-start bootstraps and populated role worlds.
/// The two initial states share the existing 512-single/512-pair work envelope.
pub fn generated_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign,matrix:bool){
    for (name,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_some()){campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("ephemeral {name}: generated memory populations and ephemeral mutation histories remain uncovered; durable campaigns use isolated hub copies"));}
    campaign.history_limit=64;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return;}
    match engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new()){
        Ok(empty)=>fixture_observers(bound,&empty,campaign),
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("empty initial campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=448;campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>if matrix{operations(bound,&fixture,campaign)}else{fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign)},
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=0;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
}
/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    column_reads::direct_fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    histories::initial_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    histories::lifecycle_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if !campaign.generated_fixture {trusted_reads(bound,fixture,campaign);}
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if campaign.generated_fixture {for witness in &mut campaign.violations[first..] {
        if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace {witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated initial fixture; fixture initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}
    }}
}

    campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>{
            column_reads::direct_fixture(bound,&fixture,campaign);
//! Validate the complete command before any project, store or host work.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#4-cli-surface-contracts — admission, every synopsis and flag help share this command table.
use std::sync::OnceLock;
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct Flag { name: String, value: String, #[serde(default)] retired: bool }
#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
struct Command { verb: String, positional: String, flags: Vec<Flag>, #[serde(skip)] allowed: String }
fn commands() -> &'static [Command] {
    static TABLE: OnceLock<Vec<Command>> = OnceLock::new();
    TABLE.get_or_init(|| {
        let mut commands: Vec<Command> = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../../packages/snapback4/bin/cli-commands.json")).expect("embedded command table");
        for command in &mut commands { command.allowed = command.flags.iter().map(|flag|flag.name.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" "); }
        commands
    })
}
fn command(verb: &str) -> Option<&'static Command> { commands().iter().find(|command|command.verb==verb) }
pub(crate) fn allowed(verb: &str) -> &'static str { command(verb).map_or("", |command| command.allowed.as_str()) }

pub fn validate(args: &[String]) -> Result<(), String> {
    let verb = args.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    // Pipe-owned private commands have positional protocols, not public flags.
    if matches!(verb, "--internal-witness-process" | "--internal-worker-watchdog") { return Ok(()); }
    let mut iter = args.iter().skip(1).peekable();
    while let Some(arg) = iter.next() {
        if arg == "--" { break; }
        if arg == "-h" { continue; }
        let Some(flag) = arg.strip_prefix("--") else {
            if arg.starts_with('-') && !arg[1..].starts_with(|c: char| c.is_ascii_digit()) {
                return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an invalid flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no {arg}") });
            }
            continue;
        };
        let (name, inline) = flag.split_once('=').map_or((flag, None), |(name, value)| (name, Some(value)));
        if name != "help" && !allowed(verb).split_whitespace().any(|known| known == name) {
            if verb == "rollback" && name == "dry-run" { return Err("E_INPUT: rollback preview is --check; use rollback <dir> [--to N|latest] --check".into()); }
            // Witness arguments may contain bearer material even in a flag name.
            return Err(if verb == "witness" { "E_INPUT: witness has an unknown flag; see guide witness".into() } else { format!("E_INPUT: {verb} takes no --{name}") });
        }
        if switch(name) {
            if inline.is_some() { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} takes no value")); }
        } else if inline.is_none() {
            // Retired spellings route to their existing no-work explanation,
            // even with a missing value; new unknown flags still refuse here.
            if retired(verb,name) {
                if iter.peek().is_some_and(|value| !value.starts_with("--")) { iter.next(); }
                continue;
            }
            let Some(value) = iter.next() else { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value")); };
            // Search text is literal, including leading dashes and punctuation.
            if value.starts_with("--") && !matches!(name, "find" | "section") {
                return Err(format!("E_INPUT: {verb} --{name} needs a value"));
            }
        }
    }
    let has = |name: &str| args.iter().skip(1).any(|arg| arg == &format!("--{name}") || arg.starts_with(&format!("--{name}=")));
    if verb == "check" {
        if has("ack-security")!=has("reason"){return Err("E_INPUT: --ack-security ID requires --reason TEXT".into());}
        if has("ack-security")&&["ack-notes","fix","store","release","emit-lean","audit"].iter().any(|f|has(f)){return Err("E_INPUT: individual security review cannot combine with --ack-notes, --fix, saved-store/release, --emit-lean or --audit".into());}
        if has("release") && !has("store") { return Err("E_INPUT: check --release requires --store <path>".into()); }
        if has("as") && !has("residency") { return Err("E_INPUT: check --as requires --residency".into()); }
        let incompatible = if has("emit-lean") { Some(("--emit-lean", &["cost","residency","fix","verbose","store","fixture","as","release","ack-notes","audit"][..])) }
            else if has("release") { Some(("--store --release", &["cost","residency","fix","fixture","as","ack-notes"][..])) } else { None };
        if let Some((mode,flags)) = incompatible {
            if let Some(flag) = flags.iter().find(|flag|has(flag)) { return Err(format!("E_INPUT: check {mode} does not accept --{flag}")); }
        }
    }
    if verb == "serve" && has("wait-stopped") && args.iter().any(|arg|arg.starts_with("--") && !matches!(arg.as_str(),"--wait-stopped"|"--help")) {
        return Err("E_INPUT: serve --wait-stopped takes only a deployment directory; it does not start or signal a server".into());
    }
    if verb == "rollback" && has("bridge") && !has("check") { return Err("E_INPUT: rollback --bridge requires --check; it writes a bridge starter without activating it".into()); }
    if verb == "check" && has("audit") && ["cost","residency","fix","store","fixture","as","release","emit-lean","ack-notes"].iter().any(|f|has(f)) { return Err("E_INPUT: check --audit takes only --json or --verbose".into()); }
    if verb == "test" && has("verbose") && !has("residency") { return Err("E_INPUT: test --verbose requires --residency".into()); }
    if verb == "run" && has("token-env") && !has("release") { return Err("E_INPUT: run --token-env requires --release".into()); }
    if verb == "why" && has("instance") && crate::verbs::parse(args).positionals.first().map(String::as_str) != Some("recent") { return Err("E_INPUT: why --instance requires why recent".into()); }
    if verb == "token" && has("port") && has("release") { return Err("E_INPUT: token --port selects dev and cannot combine with --release".into()); }
    if verb == "token" && has("quiet") && has("json") { return Err("E_INPUT: token takes either --quiet or --json".into()); }
    Ok(())
}

fn retired(verb: &str, name: &str) -> bool {
    command(verb).is_some_and(|command|command.flags.iter().any(|flag|flag.name==name && flag.retired))
}
pub fn switch(name: &str) -> bool { name=="help" || commands().iter().flat_map(|command|&command.flags).any(|flag|flag.name==name && flag.value.is_empty()) }

#[cfg(test)]
mod integration_tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn wave9_web_flags_and_upgrade_keep_shared_validation() {
        let args = ["witness", "web", "dist", "--url", "http://127.0.0.1", "--as", "alice", "--email", "alice@example.test", "--password-env", "PRIVATE_PASSWORD", "--mutation", "add", "--args", "{}", "--timeout", "30", "--export", "dist"].map(String::from);
        assert!(validate(&args).is_ok());
        for verb in ["upgrade", "witness"] {
            assert!(validate(&[verb.into(), "--unknown-wave9".into()]).is_err());
        }
        assert!(validate(&["upgrade".into(), "0.4.25".into()]).is_ok());
        assert!(validate(&["upgrade".into(), "--to".into(), "0.4.25".into(), "--json".into()]).is_ok());
        assert!(validate(&["witness".into(), "web".into(), "--password-env".into()]).is_err());
    }
}

fn value(verb: &str, name: &str) -> String {
    command(verb).and_then(|command|command.flags.iter().find(|flag|flag.name==name)).filter(|flag|!flag.value.is_empty()).map(|flag|format!(" {}",flag.value)).unwrap_or_default()
}

/// Generated from the same flag table used before dispatch and side effects.
pub(crate) fn help(verb: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::from("Flags (validated before any project or store work):\n");
    for name in allowed(verb).split_whitespace().chain(["help"]) {
        let value = value(verb, name);
        let description = match (verb, name) {
            ("deploy", "schema") => " — deploy a saved complete source; keep it outside snapback/",
            ("deploy", "staging") => " — classify a new disposable deployment as staging; existing production stores refuse",
            ("dev", "unsafe-dev-doors") => " — explicitly open unauthenticated local fixture/clock/persona doors for password-only apps",
            ("test", "source") => " — test a saved complete source; keep it outside snapback/",
            ("doctor", "repair-expiry-orphan") => " — offline recovery of one physically missing expiring parent, table:id; stop its host and back up first (guide expire)",
//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); this only orders them, names each group, and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED" | "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" | "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" | "N_CALLER_TIME" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    let priority=snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(code);
    (if priority<=2 {Tier::Review}else{tier(code)},priority)
}

        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if summary.contains("no verified complete form for this read") => "review: no verified complete form for this read; expect `capped` and show what arrived",
        "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" if form && summary.contains("the closest form is") => "fix: use the closest form below (one arm stays `complete: false`), or accept `complete: false`",

        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" if summary.contains("get NOT_FOUND") => "review: the hidden lookup runs before this require",
        "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" => "fix: this require cannot see the rows it tests",

    /// Every `.q` file under `snapback/`, `schema.q` first, as `(name, text)`.
    pub fn sources(&self) -> std::io::Result<Vec<(String, String)>> {
        let mut names: Vec<String> = std::fs::read_dir(self.schema_dir())?
            .flatten()
            .filter_map(|entry| {
                let name = entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string();
                (name.ends_with(".q") && entry.path().is_file()).then_some(name)
            })
            .collect();
        names.sort();
        if let Some(position) = names.iter().position(|n| n == "schema.q") {
            let schema = names.remove(position);

    }

//! CLI-only index labels. Canonical schema identities are never modified.
use snapback4_core::schema::{Schema, IndexComponent};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub(crate) fn indexes(schema: &Schema, text: &str, canonical: bool) -> String {
    if canonical { return text.into(); }
    let labels: BTreeMap<_, _> = schema.tables.values().flat_map(|table| table.indexes.iter())
        .filter(|(name, _)| name.starts_with("$intent:"))
        .map(|(name, index)| {
            let mut components = index.components.as_slice();
            if components.len() > 2 && matches!(components.last(), Some(IndexComponent::Column(name)) if name == "id") {
                components = &components[..components.len() - 1];
            }
            let columns = components.iter().map(|component| match component {
                IndexComponent::Column(name) => name.clone(),
                IndexComponent::Calendar(key) => {
                    let mut args = vec![key.column.clone()];
                    if key.function == "bucket" { args.push(format!("{}ms", key.millis)); }
                    if key.offset != 0 { args.push(format!("{}ms", key.offset)); }
                    format!("{}({})", key.function, args.join(", "))
                }
                IndexComponent::WordPrefixes { fields, .. } => format!("words({})", fields.join(", ")),
                IndexComponent::Contribution(_) => "contribution".into(),
            }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
            (name.as_str(), format!("index ({columns}), chosen by the planner"))
        }).collect();
    let mut out = String::new();
    let mut chars = text.char_indices().peekable();
    let mut previous = None::<char>;
    while let Some((at, c)) = chars.next() {
        // A builder's quoted literal may itself contain a canonical index
        // name. Preserve literal bytes, including escaped quotes.
        if matches!(c, '\'' | '"') && previous.is_none_or(|p| !p.is_alphanumeric()) {
            out.push(c);
            while let Some((_, next)) = chars.next() {
                out.push(next);
                if next == '\\' {
                    if let Some((_, escaped)) = chars.next() { out.push(escaped); }
                } else if next == c { break; }
            }
        } else if text[at..].starts_with("--") {
            // Source comments are opaque too: planner prose, names and quote
            // characters inside them must not affect this or following lines.
            out.push(c);
            for (_, next) in chars.by_ref() {
                out.push(next);
                if next == '\n' { break; }
            }
            previous = Some('\n');
            continue;
        } else if text[at..].starts_with("$intent:") {
            let end = text[at..].char_indices().find(|(_, c)| !c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() && !matches!(c, '$' | ':' | '_'))
                .map_or(text.len(), |(n, _)| at + n);
            let end = at + text[at..end].trim_end_matches(':').len();
            if let Some(label) = labels.get(&text[at..end]) {
                if out.ends_with('.') { out.pop(); out.push_str(" using "); }
                out.push_str(label);
                while chars.peek().is_some_and(|(n, _)| *n < end) { chars.next(); }
            } else { out.push(c); }
        } else if text[at..].starts_with("; synthesized; explicit by") {
            let end = at + "; synthesized; explicit by".len();
            while chars.peek().is_some_and(|(n, _)| *n < end) { chars.next(); }
        } else { out.push(c); }
        previous = Some(c);
    }
    out
}

/// Keep generated rule expansions readable in ordinary terminal reports.
/// Structured diagnostics and --verbose retain the entire original text.
pub(crate) fn abbreviate_quoted(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    let mut abbreviated = false;
    for (at, piece) in text.split('`').enumerate() {
        if at > 0 { out.push('`'); }
        if at % 2 == 1 && piece.chars().count() > 200 {
            out.extend(piece.chars().take(160));
            out.push('…');
            abbreviated = true;
        } else { out.push_str(piece); }
    }
    if abbreviated { out.push_str(" [expansion abbreviated; --verbose shows it in full]"); }
    out
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod compact_tests {
    #[test]
    fn long_rule_text_is_abbreviated_at_character_boundaries() {
        let text = format!("rule `{}` then `require role`", "λ or ".repeat(100));
        let compact = super::abbreviate_quoted(&text);
        assert!(compact.contains("λ") && compact.contains("--verbose") && compact.contains("`require role`"));
        assert!(compact.len() < text.len());
        assert_eq!(super::abbreviate_quoted("read <- .owner = viewer"), "read <- .owner = viewer");
    }
}

//! `check`'s advisory notes, ranked. A green check printed nine notes in
//! source order, and builders could not tell an FYI about offline coverage
//! from a page that never completes. Notes stay advisory (they never fail a
//! check); this only orders them, names each group, and shortens repeats.

#[derive(Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Debug)]
pub(crate) enum Tier {
    /// Can cost correctness or completeness: a page that never completes, a
    /// write no caller can pass, a disclosure, a read a device cannot answer.
    Review,
    /// Costs speed, meter headroom or offline coverage.
    Performance,
    /// What a declaration implies; nothing to fix for online use.
    Design,
}

pub(crate) fn tier(code: &str) -> Tier {
    match code {
        "N_IMMUTABLE_PATCH" | "N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_NO_PRODUCTION_WRITER" | "N_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT_DRIFT" | "N_REFERENCE_UNCHECKED" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED" | "N_DELETE_NEEDS_READ" | "N_PATCH_CLEARS" | "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_VACUOUS_RULE" | "N_LOOKUP_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED" | "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN" | "N_LOOKUP_NEVER_VISIBLE" | "N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN" | "N_REQUIRE_REFUSED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_SHAPE_METER" | "N_HELD_UNKNOWN" | "N_SEQUENCE_READ"
        | "N_SWEEP_DEADLINE" | "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD" | "N_PUBLIC_AGGREGATE_COPY" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" | "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"
        | "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" | "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" | "N_SEARCH_UNSCOPED" | "N_SEARCH_OPEN" | "N_TIMELINE_PROFILE_CAP" | "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" | "N_CALLER_TIME" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => Tier::Review,
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.4 — a cursorless `first N` is what the declaration says: the first N rows (wave 3 #7).
        "N_SYNC_ORDER" | "N_NO_HORIZON" | "N_MAINTAIN_SYNCED" | "N_TIMELINE_COMPAT" | "N_NO_CURSOR" => Tier::Design,
        _ => Tier::Performance,
    }
}

/// Security findings that can grant authority stay ahead of repeated advice.
pub(crate) fn summary_order(code:&str)->(Tier,u8){
    let priority=snapback4_lang::security::note_priority(code);
    (if priority<=2 {Tier::Review}else{tier(code)},priority)
}

    pub fn text_with_all(&self,all:bool)->String {

            if !all && v.status != Status::Refuted {continue;}

        }

        for (role,entries) in &self.roles {
            out.push_str(&format!("role {role} (possible/conditional; row predicates below decide):\n"));
            for entry in entries {out.push_str(&format!("  {entry}\n"));}
        }

            for path in &x.paths {out.push_str(&format!("  {path}\n"));}

        }

        if self.uncovered.is_empty(){out.push_str("  none\n");}out
    }

/// Fixture-free campaigns retain empty-start bootstraps and populated role worlds.
/// The two initial states share the existing 512-single/512-pair work envelope.
pub fn generated_campaign(bound:&Bound,campaign:&mut Campaign,matrix:bool){
    for (name,_) in bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(_,t)|t.ephemeral.is_some()){campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("ephemeral {name}: generated memory populations and ephemeral mutation histories remain uncovered; durable campaigns use isolated hub copies"));}
    campaign.history_limit=64;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
    if !campaign.reserve(1){return;}
    match engine_with(bound,&BTreeMap::new(),Vec::new()){
        Ok(empty)=>fixture_observers(bound,&empty,campaign),
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("empty initial campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=448;campaign.generated_fixture=true;
    match generated_fixture(bound,campaign){
        Ok(fixture)=>if matrix{operations(bound,&fixture,campaign)}else{fixture_observers(bound,&fixture,campaign)},
        Err(why)=>{campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("generated fixture campaign unavailable: {why}"));}
    }
    campaign.history_limit=0;campaign.generated_fixture=false;
}
/// Exercise every authored query/mutation with cross-principal candidates.
/// This is conformance exploration, not a guessed spec oracle: only declared
/// policies and replayed violations can fail security acceptance.
pub fn fixture_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,campaign:&mut Campaign){
    let first=campaign.violations.len();
    column_reads::direct_fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    histories::initial_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    histories::lifecycle_observers(bound,fixture,campaign);
    column_reads::fixture(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if !campaign.generated_fixture {trusted_reads(bound,fixture,campaign);}
    histories::run(bound,fixture,campaign);
    if campaign.generated_fixture {for witness in &mut campaign.violations[first..] {
        if witness.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace {witness.reachability=Reachability::GeneratedFixtureTrace;witness.scope="actual authored observation from a validated generated initial fixture; fixture initialization is not claimed application-reachable".into();}
    }}
}

/// The compiled form leading a note's rewrite: the text before "Otherwise:",
/// when it holds code (a lead line ending in `:` and the declaration under it).
// @ref LLP 3000.036#15.5 — the compiled owner-or-X rewrite prints by default, once per form.
pub(crate) fn compiled_form(code: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Option<(String, String)> {
    if !matches!(code, "N_NEVER_COMPLETE" | "N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE") { return None; }
    let head = rewrite.split("\n\nOtherwise:").next()?;
    let (lead, body) = head.split_once('\n')?;
    (lead.ends_with(':') && body.trim_start().starts_with("query ")).then(|| (lead.to_owned(), body.trim_end().to_owned()))
}

// Audience questions and repairs differ per rule; repeated codes cannot hide them.
fn per_rule_security(code:&str)->bool {matches!(code,"N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION"|"N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP"|"N_CALLER_TIME"|"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"|"N_BROAD_READ"|"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE")}

/// The ranked block printed after `check: ok`; empty for no notes.
pub(crate) fn render(notes: Vec<Note>) -> Vec<String> {
    if notes.is_empty() { return Vec::new(); }
    // Collapse one code's repeats at one site, keeping first-seen order.
    let mut unique: Vec<(Note, usize)> = Vec::new();
    for note in notes {
        match unique.iter_mut().find(|(seen, _)| seen.site == note.site && seen.code == note.code && seen.summary == note.summary && seen.form == note.form && seen.compact == note.compact) {
            Some((_, count)) => *count += 1,
            None => unique.push((note, 1)),
        }
    }
    let total = unique.len();
    // Rank by tier; within a tier, a code's notes stay together in the order the code first appeared.
    let first_seen = |code: &str, unique: &[(Note, usize)]| unique.iter().position(|(n, _)| n.code == code).unwrap();
    let mut order: Vec<usize> = (0..unique.len()).collect();
    order.sort_by_key(|&i| (tier(&unique[i].0.code), first_seen(&unique[i].0.code, &unique), i));
    let mut lines = vec![format!("{total} thing{} to review (advisory; check --verbose explains each, snapback4 why <CODE> one):",
        if total == 1 { "" } else { "s" })];
    let mut current = None;
    let mut shown_codes = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_forms = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_compacts = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_actions = Vec::new();
    let mut shown_context = Vec::new();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036#16.5 — wave 6 #25: a code's repeats share one line naming each subject.
    let mut repeats: Vec<(String, Vec<String>)> = Vec::new();
    let flush = |lines: &mut Vec<String>, repeats: &mut Vec<(String, Vec<String>)>| {
        for (code, subjects) in repeats.drain(..) {
            lines.push(format!("note: {code} (as above) also for {}", subjects.join("; ")));
        }
    };
    for i in order {
        let (note, count) = &unique[i];
        let group = tier(&note.code);
        if current != Some(group) { flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats); lines.push(heading(group).to_owned()); current = Some(group); }
        if note.code == "N_NO_CURSOR" {
            if !shown_codes.contains(&note.code) {
                let subjects = unique.iter().filter(|(n,_)| n.code == "N_NO_CURSOR").map(|(n,_)|
                    format!("{} ({})", subject(&n.summary), n.site)).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ");
                lines.push(format!("note: N_NO_CURSOR: no action for a top-N list. Pages without continuation: {subjects}; if the screen pages, add `c: cursor ?` and `after c`"));
                shown_codes.push(note.code.clone());
            }
            continue;
        }
        let times = if *count > 1 { format!(" (×{count} at this site)") } else { String::new() };
        let new_form = note.form.as_ref().is_some_and(|(_, code)| !shown_forms.contains(code));
        let new_compact = note.compact.as_ref().is_some_and(|parts| !shown_compacts.contains(parts));
        if shown_codes.contains(&note.code) && !new_form && !new_compact && !per_rule_security(&note.code) {
            // A repeat of a code already explained above: its subject and site, on the code's one line.
            let subject = format!("{} ({}){times}", subject(&note.summary), note.site);
            match repeats.iter_mut().find(|(code, _)| *code == note.code) {
                Some((_, subjects)) => subjects.push(subject),
                None => repeats.push((note.code.clone(), vec![subject])),
            }
            continue;
        }
        flush(&mut lines, &mut repeats);
        if let Some(parts @ [action, impact, audience]) = &note.compact {
            if !shown_actions.contains(action) {
            if audit::mentions(&json,&inv.table) && audit::authored_refusal(&json) {out.insert(format!("{}: authored refusal observations are not reduced to this read policy",p.program.name));}
        }
    }
    out.into_iter().collect()
}

pub fn add_source_notes(report:&mut Report,sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound){
    report.notes.extend(lints::source_notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(write_advice::notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(creation_advice::notes(sources,bound));
    report.notes.extend(result_advice::notes(bound));
    let mut sites=BTreeMap::new();
    for (file,source) in sources{let (doc,_)=crate::parse_document(file,source);for t in doc.tables{
        sites.insert(t.name.clone(),t.site.clone());for c in t.columns{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,c.name),c.site);}
        for r in t.rules{sites.insert(format!("{}.{}",t.name,r.effect.word()),r.site);}
    }}
    for note in &mut report.notes{if note.site.line==0 {
        let key=note.message.split([' ',':']).next().unwrap_or("");
        note.site=sites.get(key).or_else(||sites.get(key.split('.').next().unwrap_or(""))).cloned().unwrap_or_else(||Site::new(sources.first().map(|s|s.0.as_str()).unwrap_or("schema.q"),1,1));
    }}
}

//! Mutable syntax traversal shared by planning and final index-name resolution.
use crate::ast::*;

pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    match value {
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read_children(read, visit),
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expression(left, visit); expression(right, visit); }
        Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => { for item in items { expression(item, visit); } }
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => { for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } } }
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => { expression(base, visit); shape(fields, visit); }
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => { expression(then, visit); expression(condition, visit); expression(otherwise, visit); }
        Expr::Walk { seed, .. } => expression(seed, visit),
        Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => {
            for key in keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
            for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } }
            if let Some(value) = condition { expression(value, visit); }
        }
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            for value in [&mut merge.matching, &mut merge.filter, &mut merge.cursor].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
            let mut lanes = Vec::new();
            for mut lane in std::mem::take(&mut merge.lanes) {
                if let Some(predicate) = &mut lane.admission { expression(predicate, visit); }
                if let Some((_, read)) = &mut lane.nulls {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    let Expr::Read(planned) = value else { unreachable!("planned null scan") };
                    **read = planned;
                }
                if let Some(value) = &mut lane.distinct { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(fields) = &mut lane.project { shape(fields, visit); }
                for value in &mut lane.prefix { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some((_, value)) = &mut lane.loop_ { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(read) = &mut lane.planned {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    match value {
                        Expr::Read(planned) => **read = planned,
                        Expr::Merge(nested) if lane.loop_.is_none() => {
                            for mut child in nested.lanes {
                                child.branch = lane.branch.clone();
                                child.project = lane.project.clone();
                                lanes.push(child);
                            }
                            continue;
                        }
                        _ => unreachable!("a planned lane is a read or an expanded read"),
                    }
                }
                lanes.push(lane);
            }
            merge.lanes = lanes;
        }
        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => {}
    }
    visit(value);
}

fn read_children(read: &mut ReadExpr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for key in &mut read.keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    for seek in &mut read.seeks { expression(&mut seek.value, visit); }
    for value in [&mut read.filter, &mut read.matching].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    if let Some(value) = read.range.as_mut().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_mut()) { expression(value, visit); }
}

pub(super) fn shape(fields: &mut [ShapeField], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for field in fields { if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { expression(value, visit); } }
}

pub(super) fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for stmt in body {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Bind { value, .. } | Stmt::Expr { value, .. } | Stmt::Return { value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, visit),
            Stmt::Emit { payload, once, .. } => {
                expression(payload, visit);
                if let Some(key) = once { expression(key, visit); }
            }
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { expression(condition, visit); statements(then_body, visit); statements(else_body, visit); }
            Stmt::As { principal: domain, body, .. } | Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => { expression(domain, visit); statements(body, visit); }
            Stmt::Break { .. } | Stmt::Continue { .. } => {}
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn document(doc: &mut Document, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — historical lookups receive the same predicate/index planning as ordinary reads.
    for fill in doc.backfills.iter_mut().chain(&mut doc.downfills) {expression(&mut fill.value,visit);}

    for program in &mut doc.programs { statements(&mut program.prelude, visit); statements(&mut program.body, visit); }
    for function in &mut doc.functions {
        match &mut function.body { FunctionBody::Expression(value) => expression(value, visit), FunctionBody::Block(body) => statements(body, visit) }
    }
    for decl in &mut doc.shapes { shape(&mut decl.fields, visit); }
}
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#163-directed-finite-histories — pruning orders finite replay, never proves safety.
pub(super) fn lifecycle_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    if bound.invariants.is_empty()||out.exhausted()||bound.invariants.iter().all(|i|out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==i.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){return;}

        let t=&bound.schema.tables[&i.table];let gate=match i.target.as_str(){"read"=>super::super::columns::admission(t,i.column.as_deref()),"insert"=>t.rules.insert.clone(),"update"=>t.update_rule_judged().into_owned(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow};
        i.policy.as_ref().is_none_or(|policy|kernel::implies(&gate,policy)!=Ok(true))
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if interesting.is_empty(){return;}
    let mut relevant=interesting.into_iter().flat_map(|i|std::iter::once(i.table.clone()).chain(i.policy.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies))).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    for _ in 0..bound.schema.tables.len(){let old=relevant.clone();for name in &old{for kind in bound.schema.tables[name].columns.values(){if let C::Ref{table}=kind.required(){relevant.insert(table.clone());}}}if old==relevant{break;}}
    let has_lifecycle=relevant.iter().any(|name|bound.schema.tables[name].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..})&&!super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).contains(c)));
    if !has_lifecycle{return;}

    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();

    fn signature(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->String{
        // Equality of this projection is only an exploration heuristic. Missing
        // worlds are explicitly uncovered; it never discharges an obligation.
        let projected=rows.iter().map(|(name,rs)|{let t=&bound.schema.tables[name];let mut images=rs.iter().map(|r|t.columns.iter().filter(|(c,k)|c.as_str()!="id"&&matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..}|C::Bool|C::Principal|C::Ref{..})).filter_map(|(c,_)|r.field(c).ok().map(|v|(c.clone(),v.clone()))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>()).collect::<Vec<_>>();images.sort_by_key(|r|serde_json::to_string(r).unwrap());(name.clone(),images)}).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();serde_json::to_string(&projected).unwrap()
    }

        let mut next=Vec::new();

            let Ok(before)=readback(&base)else{continue;};
            for bp in &transitions{
                let p=&bp.program;

                let program_json=serde_json::to_string(p).unwrap();
}
// Prefer real lifecycle successors before the general diagonal history matrix.
// Values copied by creators are never replaced with invented terminal states.
pub(super) fn lifecycle_observers(bound:&Bound,fixture:&Engine,out:&mut Campaign){
    let mut relevant=BTreeSet::new();
    fn lifecycle(bound:&Bound,source:&str,rule:&RuleExpr,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
        RuleExpr::Exists{table,index,key,equalities,..}=>{
            if super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,table).is_empty()&&super::super::privilege::probe_pairs(bound,table,index,key,equalities).iter().any(|(c,term)|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Literal(_))&&bound.schema.tables[table].columns.get(c).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..}))){out.insert(table.clone());}
        },
        RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)|RuleExpr::Compare(_,a,b)=>{
            if super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,source).is_empty()&&[a,b].iter().any(|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::Field(c)|RuleTerm::NextField(c)|RuleTerm::OldField(c) if bound.schema.tables[source].columns.get(c).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..})))){out.insert(source.into());}
        },
        RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for r in xs{lifecycle(bound,source,r,out)},RuleExpr::Not(r)=>lifecycle(bound,source,r,out),_=>{}
    }}
    for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.target=="read"&&!out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==i.site.to_string()&&v.reachability.is_reachable())){
        if let Some(p)=&inv.policy{lifecycle(bound,&inv.table,p,&mut relevant);}
    }
    if relevant.is_empty(){return;}
    let transitions=bound.programs.iter().filter(|bp|bp.program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&!bp.program.native&&!bound.schema.operations.get(&bp.program.name).is_some_and(|f|f.ephemeral)).filter(|bp|{
        let json=serde_json::to_string(&bp.program).unwrap();
        ir::write_tables(&bp.program).iter().any(|(name,kind)|*kind=="update"&&relevant.contains(name)&&super::super::privilege::role_columns(bound,name).is_empty()&&bound.schema.tables[name].columns.iter().any(|(c,k)|matches!(k.required(),C::Enum{..})&&json.contains(&format!("\"{c}\":"))))
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if transitions.is_empty()||out.exhausted(){return;}
    let Ok(initial)=readback(fixture)else{return;};
    let actors=actors(bound,&initial);
    if !out.reserve(1){return;}
    let Ok(root)=fixture.fork_fixture()else{return;};
    let mut frontier=vec![(root,Vec::<Step>::new())];
    let mut seen=BTreeSet::from([serde_json::to_string(&initial).unwrap()]);
    let mut visited=0;
    for _ in 0..3{
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        for (base,steps) in frontier{
            let Ok(before)=readback(&base)else{continue;};
            for bp in &transitions{
                let p=&bp.program;
                let cases=fixture_cases(bound,p,&before,&actors,out);
                if cases.len()>32{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: lifecycle arguments capped at 32",p.name));}
                for args in cases.into_iter().take(32){for viewer in &actors{
                    if visited>=256||!out.reserve(1){out.unsupported.insert("lifecycle successors capped at 256 attempts; remaining state transitions uncovered".into());return;}visited+=1;
                    let Ok(mut engine)=base.fork_fixture()else{continue;};
                    let Ok(args)=materialize_arguments(&mut engine,&p.args,&args,viewer)else{continue;};
                    out.attempted+=1;*out.operations.entry(p.name.clone()).or_default()+=1;
                    let trace=super::executed::trace(bound,p);
                    let Ok(result)=engine.mutate(&p.name,None,Context::new(viewer,args.clone()).at(NOW).with_fixture_trace(trace.clone()))else{continue;};

-- Food Truck Permits: city health department permits for food trucks.
--
-- Roles: operator (truck owners), inspector (field inspections and the live
-- board), supervisor (environmental health supervisor: suspends permits and
-- approves operators with a past suspension, recording the reason).
-- Role rows are provisioned by the local operator (service:operator), the
-- same pattern as `guide staging`: signup alone grants no role.
use identity password

table roster:
  principal: principal
  role: enum('operator', 'inspector', 'supervisor')
  unique principal
  immutable principal
  read <- .principal = viewer or viewer = 'service:operator'
  insert <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  update <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  delete <- viewer = 'service:operator'
  online only

mutation provision(principal: principal, role: enum('operator', 'inspector', 'supervisor')):
  require viewer = 'service:operator' else NOT_OPERATOR
  return upsert roster[principal] { principal, role }

query myRole():
  return roster[viewer]


-- Trucks belong to exactly one operator. activePermit points at the truck's
-- current permit once one is issued (maintained by approveApplication).
table trucks:
  owner: principal
  name: text 1..80
  licensePlate: text 1..16
  activePermit: permits ?
  unique licensePlate else PLATE_TAKEN
  immutable owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation registerTruck(name: text 1..80, licensePlate: text 1..16):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  return insert trucks { owner: viewer, name, licensePlate }

query myTrucks(c: cursor ?):
  return trucks where .owner = viewer first 50 by .id after c

query allTrucks(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return trucks first 50 by .id after c


-- Each application must include interior photos, the fire extinguisher tag
-- photo and the permit fee: all three columns are required, so an
-- application row cannot exist without them.
table applications:
  truck: trucks
  owner: principal
  interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4
  extinguisherTag: image <=5000000
  feeCents: int 1..100000000
  status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'approved', 'failed') default 'applied'
  failedAt: time default now
  lastPassed: bool default false
  createdAt: time default now
  immutable truck, owner, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents, createdAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation submitApplication(truck: trucks, interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4, extinguisherTag: image <=5000000, feeCents: int 1..100000000):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  t = trucks[truck] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require t.owner = viewer else NOT_YOUR_TRUCK
  flag = operatorFlags[viewer]
  require flag = null or flag.suspended = false else OPERATOR_SUSPENDED
  return insert applications { truck, owner: viewer, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents }

query myApplications(c: cursor ?):
  return applications where .owner = viewer first 50 by .createdAt after c

-- The live board: every application as it moves applied -> inspected ->
-- approved / failed. Staff only.
query board(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return applications first 50 by .createdAt after c

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/diag.rs:36:pub struct Diagnostic {
//! Author-owned intent starts with a reviewable draft, never inferred policy.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#103-adoption — syntactic coverage is distinct from a proved invariant.
use super::*;

pub(super) fn missing(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    bound.schema.visible_tables().filter(|(name,t)| {
        ([&t.rules.read,&t.rules.insert,&t.rules.update,&t.rules.delete].iter().any(|r|!matches!(r,RuleExpr::Deny)) || !t.written_by.is_empty())
            && !bound.invariants.iter().any(|i| &i.table==*name)
    }).map(|(name,_)|name.clone()).collect()
}

/// Specification questions, never proposed enforcement or accepted policy.
/// The caller must keep this outside the compiled .q source set.
pub fn draft(bound:&Bound)->String{
    let mut out=String::from("-- DRAFT: NOT ACCEPTED. Answer from the APP SPECIFICATION, not from the rules.\n-- Each answer needs an audience, a resource relationship and lifecycle/consent conditions.\n-- Use table-read intent for a shared row audience; add column-read intent for distinct privacy boundaries.\n-- Assess every role and column, without automatically declaring one invariant for each question.\n-- Identify each role's positive product/workflow grant before retaining access to other people's private data.\n-- Write confirmed declarations in authored snapback/*.q, then run check -> test -> audit.\n-- This questionnaire is never compiled and does not propose policy.\n-- snapback4 guide security\n\n");
    let roles=super::intent::roles(bound);
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){
        out.push_str(&format!("-- {name}: {}\n",super::intent::row_context(t)));
        let audiences=std::iter::once("a signed-in user without a roster role (check own rows and unrelated rows separately)".to_owned())
            .chain(roles.iter().cloned()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        for audience in &audiences {
            out.push_str(&format!("-- Q {name}.write / {audience}: may any user of this role write ANY {name} row, or only the owner of THIS resource? Which relationship and state must hold?\n"));
            out.push_str(&format!("-- Q {name}.read / {audience}: which rows may this audience see according to the spec? Own, assigned, team-wide, or none? Which state/consent conditions must hold now?\n"));
        }
        for (column,kind) in &t.columns {
            if column=="id"||t.engine_field(column){continue;}
            for audience in &audiences {
                out.push_str(&format!("-- Q {name}.{column} ({kind}) / {audience}: may this audience read this value, including downloads and derived values? On which related rows and in which states? If not, which independently confirmed column-read invariant excludes it?\n"));
            }
        }
        for inv in bound.invariants.iter().filter(|i|i.table==*name&&i.column.is_some()&&i.target=="update"){
            out.push_str(&format!("-- {}: update-only intent excludes creation. Does the specification allow callers to supply this column at insert/upsert too? Use write only if creation shares the restriction.\n",inv.target_label()));
        }
        for effect in ["insert","update","delete"] {
            if covered(bound,name,effect){continue;}
            out.push_str(&format!("-- Q {name}.{effect}: who may {effect}, who appoints that authority, and which relationship/state must hold? Can callers claim the relationship themselves?\n"));
        }
        for (column,authority) in &t.written_by {out.push_str(&format!("-- Q {name}.{column}: may .{} edit this value in every state? Column update intent covers edits; write also covers creation.\n",authority.owner));}
        out.push('\n');
    }
    out
}

fn covered(bound:&Bound,table:&str,effect:&str)->bool{
    bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==table && i.operation.is_none() && i.column.is_none()
        && (i.target==effect || i.target=="write" && matches!(effect,"insert"|"update")))
}
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize)]
pub struct UncoveredExposure {pub table:String,pub effect:String,pub audience:String,pub row_predicate:String,pub column:Option<String>}
pub(super) fn uncovered(bound:&Bound,exposures:&[Exposure])->Vec<UncoveredExposure>{
    let mut out=exposures.iter().flat_map(|x|x.effects.iter().filter(|(effect,rule)|rule.as_str()!="deny"&&!covered(bound,&x.table,effect)).map(|(effect,predicate)|UncoveredExposure{
        table:x.table.clone(),effect:effect.clone(),audience:if predicate=="allow"||predicate.starts_with("public"){"every signed-in user".into()}else{format!("caller satisfying {predicate}")},row_predicate:predicate.clone(),column:None,
    })).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    for (name,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (column,authority) in &t.written_by{
        if covered(bound,name,"update")||bound.invariants.iter().any(|i|i.table==*name&&i.operation.is_none()&&i.column.as_ref()==Some(column)&&matches!(i.target.as_str(),"write"|"update")){continue;}
        out.push(UncoveredExposure{table:name.clone(),effect:"update".into(),column:Some(column.clone()),audience:format!("owner .{} = viewer",authority.owner),row_predicate:format!("field-owner patch authority for {column}; table update rule may deny")});
    }}out
}

/// The operation or table a repeat is about (`query b`), else its short subject.
fn subject(summary: &str) -> String {
    let mut words = summary.splitn(3, ' ');
    match (words.next(), words.next()) {
        (Some(kind @ ("query" | "mutation" | "job" | "action" | "table" | "view")), Some(name)) => format!("{kind} {}", name.trim_end_matches(':')),
        _ => short(summary),
    }
}

/// The subject of a repeated note: its first clause before any quoted source, at most 100 characters.
fn short(summary: &str) -> String {
//! Diagnostics: one envelope, located, with a rewrite when a safe repair is known
//! and the keyword card to read (LLP 2000.000 §5.1).

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::fmt;

#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Site {
    pub file: String,
    pub line: u32,
    pub col: u32,
}

impl Site {
    pub fn new(file: &str, line: u32, col: u32) -> Self {
        Self { file: file.to_owned(), line, col }
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for Site {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        write!(f, "{}:{}:{}", self.file, self.line, self.col)
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "snake_case")]
pub enum SourceRewriteKind {
    MixedKey,
    NamedUpdate,
    WriteResultNarrowing,
    MembershipBootstrap,
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Diagnostic {
    pub site: Site,
    pub code: String,
    pub message: String,
    pub rewrite: String,
    /// Reservation evidence retained for JSON and check --verbose.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub details: Option<String>,
    /// Exact Quarry source that may replace the diagnostic's authored
    /// expression or rule. Most rewrites are design guidance and leave this
    /// unset; golden tests compile only this compiler-emitted source.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub source_rewrite: Option<String>,
    /// Exhaustive category used by the rewrite golden. Adding a new concrete
    /// rule edit requires adding a category and a compile/semantics case.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub source_rewrite_kind: Option<SourceRewriteKind>,
    pub guide: String,
    /// An opt-in `check --fix` edit: spelling repairs or a verified positional-key choice.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub mechanical: bool,
    /// Parser-proven consequence of an earlier error in this operation,
    /// with no independent information. Kept for verbose and JSON reports.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub cascade_of: Option<Site>,
    /// Compiler-only candidate; source is withheld until contextual verification.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) read_repair: Option<crate::repair::ReadRepair>,
    /// Exact original span proved by the compiler, never part of wire diagnostics.
    #[serde(skip)]
    pub(crate) verified_edit: Option<(Site, String)>,
}

impl Diagnostic {
    pub fn new(site: &Site, code: &str, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        let code = code.to_owned();
        let guide = guide_for(&code).to_owned();
        Self { site: site.clone(), code, message: message.into(), rewrite: rewrite.into(), details: None, source_rewrite: None, source_rewrite_kind: None, guide, mechanical: false, cascade_of: None, read_repair: None, verified_edit: None }
    }

    pub fn details(mut self, details: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.details = Some(details.into());
        self
    }

    /// The CLI must match this original source before applying `rewrite`.
    pub fn verified_edit(&self) -> Option<(&Site, &str)> {
        self.verified_edit.as_ref().map(|(site, source)| (site, source.as_str()))
    }

    pub(crate) fn repair_read(mut self, site: &Site, changes: Vec<crate::repair::ReadChange>) -> Self {
        self.rewrite.clear();
        self.read_repair = Some(crate::repair::ReadRepair { site: site.clone(), changes });
        self
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#8 — only explicitly mechanical rewrites may change source
    pub fn mechanical(mut self) -> Self {
        self.mechanical = true;
        self
    }

    /// Attach a source fragment whose syntax and meaning have been checked in
    /// this diagnostic's context. This does not make the edit mechanical.
    pub(crate) fn source_rewrite(mut self, kind: SourceRewriteKind, source: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        self.source_rewrite_kind = Some(kind);
        self.source_rewrite = Some(source.into());
        self
    }

    pub fn guide(mut self, guide: &str) -> Self {
        self.guide = guide.to_owned();
        self
    }

    /// A repeated diagnostic gets inline help only if its card discusses it.
    pub fn explanation_section(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let keyword = if self.guide.is_empty() { guide_for(&self.code) } else { &self.guide };
        crate::guide::explanation_section(&self.code, keyword)
    }

    /// A contextual diagnostic owns its card; a code alone uses the default.
    pub fn explanation(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let keyword = if self.guide.is_empty() { guide_for(&self.code) } else { &self.guide };
        crate::guide::explain_code_with_guide(&self.code, keyword)
    }
}

impl fmt::Display for Diagnostic {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
        let discovery = if self.mechanical { " — check --fix applies it" } else { "" };
        write!(f, "{} — {}: {}", self.site, self.code, self.message)?;
        if !self.rewrite.is_empty() { write!(f, "\n  rewrite: {}{discovery}", self.rewrite)?; }
        write!(f, "\n  guide: {}", self.guide)
    }
}

pub type Fallible<T> = Result<T, Diagnostic>;

pub fn syntax(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX", message, "fix this Quarry line and rerun snapback4 check")
}

pub fn syntax_with(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX", message, rewrite)
}

pub fn unsupported(site: &Site, construct: &str, alternative: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    let mut diagnostic = Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_UNSUPPORTED", format!("{construct} is not part of Snapback 4"), alternative);
    diagnostic.mechanical = matches!(construct, "elif" | "? :" | "?." | "len" | "//" | "#" | "proc");
    diagnostic
}

pub fn unknown(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    let diagnostic = Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_UNKNOWN", message, rewrite);
    if diagnostic.message.contains(" has no index ") && diagnostic.rewrite.starts_with("by ") && !diagnostic.rewrite.contains('<') {
        diagnostic.mechanical()
    } else {
        diagnostic
    }
}

pub fn ambiguous(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_AMBIGUOUS", message, rewrite).mechanical()
}

pub fn type_error(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_TYPE", message, rewrite)
}

pub fn placement(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_QUARRY_PLACEMENT", message, rewrite)
}

pub fn unbound_loop(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_UNBOUND_LOOP", message, rewrite)
}

pub fn unbound_scan(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_UNBOUND_SCAN", message, rewrite)
}

pub fn sync_error(site: &Site, message: impl Into<String>, rewrite: impl Into<String>) -> Diagnostic {
    Diagnostic::new(site, "E_SYNC", message, rewrite)
}

        _ => "language",
    }

/// "did you mean `x`?" for a misspelled name, from the declared names.
pub fn name_rewrite<'a>(name: &str, declared: impl IntoIterator<Item = &'a str>, repair: Option<&str>) -> String {
    let mut names: Vec<&str> = declared.into_iter().collect();
    names.sort_unstable();
    names.dedup();
    let lower = name.to_lowercase();
    let mut ranked: Vec<(&str, usize)> = names
        .iter()
        .filter(|candidate| candidate.len().abs_diff(name.len()) <= 2 || candidate.to_lowercase() == lower)
        .map(|candidate| (*candidate, distance(name, candidate)))
        .filter(|(candidate, distance)| *distance <= 2 || candidate.to_lowercase() == lower)
        .collect();
    ranked.sort_by(|a, b| (b.0.to_lowercase() == lower).cmp(&(a.0.to_lowercase() == lower)).then(a.1.cmp(&b.1)).then(a.0.cmp(b.0)));
    let suggestion = ranked.first().map(|(candidate, _)| *candidate);
    let names_text = match suggestion {
        Some(suggestion) => format!("did you mean `{suggestion}`?"),
        None => format!("declared names: {}", if names.is_empty() { "none".to_owned() } else { names.join(", ") }),
    };
    match repair {
        Some(repair) => format!("{names_text}; {repair}"),
        None => names_text,
    }
}

/// Damerau–Levenshtein distance with adjacent transpositions.
fn distance(left: &str, right: &str) -> usize {
    let a: Vec<char> = left.chars().collect();
    let b: Vec<char> = right.chars().collect();
    let max = a.len() + b.len();
    let mut d = vec![vec![0usize; b.len() + 2]; a.len() + 2];
    d[0][0] = max;
    for i in 0..=a.len() {
        d[i + 1][0] = max;
        d[i + 1][1] = i;
    }
    for j in 0..=b.len() {
        d[0][j + 1] = max;
        d[1][j + 1] = j;
    }
    let mut last: std::collections::HashMap<char, usize> = std::collections::HashMap::new();
    for i in 1..=a.len() {
        let mut matched = 0;
        for j in 1..=b.len() {
            let row = *last.get(&b[j - 1]).unwrap_or(&0);
            let col = matched;
            let cost = usize::from(a[i - 1] != b[j - 1]);
            if cost == 0 {
                matched = j;
            }
            d[i + 1][j + 1] = (d[i][j] + cost).min(d[i + 1][j] + 1).min(d[i][j + 1] + 1).min(d[row][col] + (i - row - 1) + 1 + (j - col - 1));
        }
        last.insert(a[i - 1], i);
    }
    d[a.len() + 1][b.len() + 1]
}

mod tests {

    #[test]
    fn suggests_the_nearest_name() {
        assert_eq!(name_rewrite("conversationid", ["conversationId", "creatorId"], None), "did you mean `conversationId`?");
        assert_eq!(name_rewrite("zzz", ["a"], Some("declare it")), "declared names: a; declare it");
    }
}
